            newHum.Parent = char
            if workspace.CurrentCamera then
                workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = newHum
            end
            if hum.Parent then hum:Destroy() end
        end)
        if ad.enabled then
            M.activateAntiDie(char)
        end
    end)
end

function M.applyAntiReset()
    pcall(function()
        game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("ResetButtonCallback", false)
    end)
end

function M.restoreReset()
    pcall(function()
        game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("ResetButtonCallback", true)
    end)
end

function M.startAntiDie()
    local ad = M._antiDie
    ad.enabled = true
    M.antiDieEnabled = true

    if ad.loop then pcall(function() ad.loop:Disconnect() end) ad.loop = nil end
    if ad.charConn then pcall(function() ad.charConn:Disconnect() end) ad.charConn = nil end

    ad.loop = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not ad.enabled then return end
        local char = player.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hum then return end
        _adPreventDamage(root, hum)
    end)

    ad.charConn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        if not ad.enabled then return end
        task.defer(function()
            M.activateAntiDie(char)
        end)
        task.spawn(function()
            for _ = 1, 12 do
                if not ad.enabled then return end
                M.applyAntiReset()
                task.wait(0.25)
            end
        end)
    end)

    task.defer(function()
        M.activateAntiDie(player.Character)
    end)

    M.applyAntiReset()
    task.spawn(function()
        for _ = 1, 12 do
            if not ad.enabled then return end
            M.applyAntiReset()
            task.wait(0.25)
        end
    end)
end

function M.stopAntiDie()
    local ad = M._antiDie
    ad.enabled = false
    M.antiDieEnabled = false

    if ad.loop then pcall(function() ad.loop:Disconnect() end) ad.loop = nil end
    if ad.charConn then pcall(function() ad.charConn:Disconnect() end) ad.charConn = nil end

    local hum = ad.humanoid
    _adDisconnectHumanoid()
    if hum and hum.Parent then
        pcall(function()
            hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = true
            hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
        end)
    end

    M.restoreReset()
end

-- ============================================================
-- ANTI VOID (Adapt port)
-- Saves last safe CFrame on ground; teleports back when below void
-- ============================================================
M.antiVoidEnabled = false
M._antiVoidConn = nil
M._antiVoidSafeCFrame = nil

function M.antiVoidSaveSafe()
    local char = player.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hum or not root or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
    if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
        M._antiVoidSafeCFrame = root.CFrame
    end
end

function M.stopAntiVoid()
    if M._antiVoidConn then
        pcall(function() M._antiVoidConn:Disconnect() end)
        M._antiVoidConn = nil
    end
    M.antiVoidEnabled = false
    M.antiFlingEnabled = false
    M.noPlayerCollisionEnabled = false
end

function M.startAntiVoid()
    M.stopAntiVoid()
    M.antiVoidEnabled = true
    M.antiVoidSaveSafe()

    M._antiVoidConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not M.antiVoidEnabled then return end
        local char = player.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hum or not root or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

        local voidY = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight + 30
        if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air and root.Position.Y > voidY + 12 then
            M._antiVoidSafeCFrame = root.CFrame
        elseif root.Position.Y <= voidY and M._antiVoidSafeCFrame then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.CFrame = M._antiVoidSafeCFrame + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
            hum.PlatformStand = false
            hum.Sit = false
            pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end)
        end
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
-- ANTI FLING (K7 port) — clamp linear/angular velocity spikes
-- ============================================================
M.antiFlingEnabled = false
M._antiFlingConn = nil
M.ANTI_FLING_MAX_LIN = 160
M.ANTI_FLING_MAX_ANG = 45
M.ANTI_FLING_AIMBOT_LIN = 220
M.ANTI_FLING_AIMBOT_ANG = 60

function M._antiFlingApply(root, char, maxLin, maxAng)
    if not root or not root.Parent then return end
    pcall(function()
        local v = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local horiz = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
        local hMag = horiz.Magnitude
        if hMag > maxLin then
            local scale = maxLin / hMag
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X * scale, v.Y, v.Z * scale)
        elseif v.Magnitude > maxLin * 1.35 then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = v.Unit * maxLin
        end
        local av = root.AssemblyAngularVelocity
        if av.Magnitude > maxAng then
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        if char then
            for _, p in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                if p:IsA("BasePart") and p ~= root then
                    local pv = p.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                    local ph = Vector3.new(pv.X, 0, pv.Z)
                    if ph.Magnitude > maxLin * 1.2 then
                        local scale = (maxLin * 1.05) / ph.Magnitude
                        p.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(pv.X * scale, pv.Y, pv.Z * scale)
                    end
                    if p.AssemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude > maxAng then
                        p.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function M.startAntiFling()
    if M._antiFlingConn then return end
    M.antiFlingEnabled = true
    M._antiFlingConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not M.antiFlingEnabled then return end
        local char = player.Character
        if not char then return end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then return end
        local aimOn = M.autoBatEnabled or M.bypassAimbotEnabled
        local maxLin = aimOn and M.ANTI_FLING_AIMBOT_LIN or M.ANTI_FLING_MAX_LIN
        local maxAng = aimOn and M.ANTI_FLING_AIMBOT_ANG or M.ANTI_FLING_MAX_ANG
        M._antiFlingApply(root, char, maxLin, maxAng)
    end)
end

function M.stopAntiFling()
    M.antiFlingEnabled = false
    if M._antiFlingConn then
        pcall(function() M._antiFlingConn:Disconnect() end)
        M._antiFlingConn = nil
    end
end

-- ============================================================
-- NO PLAYER COLLISION (K7 port)
-- Only disables OTHER players' parts locally — never local char group
-- ============================================================
M.noPlayerCollisionEnabled = false
M._noPlayerColConn = nil
M._noPlayerColHB = nil

function M.applyNoPlayerCollision()
    local function stripOther(char)
        if not char then return end
        for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                pcall(function() part.CanCollide = false end)
            end
        end
    end
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= player and plr.Character then stripOther(plr.Character) end
    end
    if not M._noPlayerColConn then
        M._noPlayerColConn = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
            plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
                task.wait(0.15)
                if M.noPlayerCollisionEnabled then stripOther(char) end
            end)
            if plr.Character and M.noPlayerCollisionEnabled then stripOther(plr.Character) end
        end)
    end
    if not M._noPlayerColHB then
        M._noPlayerColHB = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not M.noPlayerCollisionEnabled then return end
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= player and plr.Character then
                    for _, part in ipairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
                        if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.CanCollide then
                            part.CanCollide = false
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

function M.startNoPlayerCollision()
    M.noPlayerCollisionEnabled = true
    M.applyNoPlayerCollision()
end

function M.stopNoPlayerCollision()
    M.noPlayerCollisionEnabled = false
    -- leave connections; Heartbeat early-outs when disabled
end

-- ============================================================
-- ANTI DROP V2 (May Duels port — read-only velocity spoof)
-- Architecture:
--   __index  → external readers see clamped spoof (WalkSpeed cap)
--   __newindex → external writes captured into spoof buffer only
--   PreSimulation → READ real HRP velocity, clamp horizontal to WalkSpeed,
--                   update spoof buffer. NEVER writes to HRP.
-- Impact: zero mutation of movement pipeline (NS/CS/Lagger intact);
-- prevents drop detectors that sample Velocity/AssemblyLinearVelocity.
-- Toggle: Mechanics → Protection → Anti Die → Anti Drop
-- ============================================================
M.antiDropEnabled = false
M._antiDropSpoofedVel = Vector3.zero
M._antiDropPreSimConn = nil
M._antiDropHooksInstalled = false

function M.installAntiDropHooks()
    if M._antiDropHooksInstalled then return end
    if type(hookmetamethod) ~= "function" or type(newcclosure) ~= "function" or type(checkcaller) ~= "function" then
        warn("[FLOW] Anti Drop V2: executor does not support hookmetamethod — feature disabled")
        return
    end
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        local oldIndex
        oldIndex = hookmetamethod(game, "__index", newcclosure(function(self, key)
            if M.antiDropEnabled and not checkcaller() and (key == "AssemblyLinearVelocity" or key == "Velocity") then
                if typeof(self) == "Instance" and self:IsA("BasePart")
                    and self.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then
                    local char = player.Character
                    if char and self:IsDescendantOf(char) then
                        return M._antiDropSpoofedVel
                    end
                end
            end
            return oldIndex(self, key)
        end))

        local oldNewIndex
        oldNewIndex = hookmetamethod(game, "__newindex", newcclosure(function(self, key, value)
            if M.antiDropEnabled and not checkcaller() and (key == "AssemblyLinearVelocity" or key == "Velocity") then
                if typeof(self) == "Instance" and self:IsA("BasePart")
                    and self.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then
                    local char = player.Character
                    if char and self:IsDescendantOf(char) then
                        -- Capture external write into spoof only; do not touch real velocity
                        if typeof(value) == "Vector3" then
                            M._antiDropSpoofedVel = value
                        end
                        return
                    end
                end
            end
            return oldNewIndex(self, key, value)
        end))
    end)
    if ok then
        M._antiDropHooksInstalled = true
    else
        warn("[FLOW] Anti Drop V2 hooks failed: " .. tostring(err))
    end
end

-- Read-only sampler: mirrors real velocity into spoof with WalkSpeed clamp.
-- Critical path: PreSimulation only; no HRP write → zero interaction with
-- applySpeedMethod / getActiveMoveSpeed / Lagger pipelines.
function M.applyAntiDropVelocity()
    if not M.antiDropEnabled then return end
    local char = player.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not (char and hum and root) then return end

    local real = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    local flat = Vector3.new(real.X, 0, real.Z)
    local walk = hum.WalkSpeed
    if walk <= 0 then walk = 16 end
    if flat.Magnitude > walk then
        flat = flat.Unit * walk
    end
    M._antiDropSpoofedVel = Vector3.new(flat.X, real.Y, flat.Z)
end

function M.startAntiDrop()
    M.installAntiDropHooks()
    if not M._antiDropHooksInstalled then
        M.antiDropEnabled = false
        if M.setAntiDropVisual then pcall(function() M.setAntiDropVisual(false) end) end
        warn("[FLOW] Anti Drop V2 unavailable on this executor")
        return
    end
    M.antiDropEnabled = true
    if M._antiDropPreSimConn then
        pcall(function() M._antiDropPreSimConn:Disconnect() end)
        M._antiDropPreSimConn = nil
    end
    M._antiDropPreSimConn = RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(function()
        M.applyAntiDropVelocity()
    end)
end

function M.stopAntiDrop()
    M.antiDropEnabled = false
    if M._antiDropPreSimConn then
        pcall(function() M._antiDropPreSimConn:Disconnect() end)
        M._antiDropPreSimConn = nil
    end
    M._antiDropSpoofedVel = Vector3.zero
end


-- ============================================================
-- AIMBOT (portado do Adapt Duels) — Normal + Anti Bypass + TP Bat (Anti Desync)
-- ============================================================
M.autoBatEnabled = false
M.aimbotMode = M.aimbotMode or "Normal" -- "Normal" | "Anti Bypass"
M.AIMBOT_SPEED = tonumber(M.AIMBOT_SPEED) or 58
M.LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED = tonumber(M.LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED) or 40
M.antiBypassAimbotSpeed = tonumber(M.antiBypassAimbotSpeed) or 58
M.antiBypassLaggerAimbotSpeed = tonumber(M.antiBypassLaggerAimbotSpeed) or 40
M.aimbotConn = nil
M._aimbotLV = nil
M._aimbotAtt = nil
M._aimbotTarget = nil
M._aimbotLastScan = 0
M._aimbotSwingCooldown = false
M._autoTPWasEnabledForBat = false

M.bypassAimbotEnabled = false -- TP Bat / Anti Desync
M._antiDesyncConn = nil
M._antiDesyncHitCD = false
M._antiDesyncHRP = nil
M._antiDesyncHum = nil

local ADAPT_AB_SETTINGS = {
    SPEED = 63, VERT_SPEED = 52, DISTANCE = -2.8, HEIGHT = 4.75,
    V_OFFSET = 1, TURN_SPEED = 285, MAX_TURN_RATE = 28, AUTO_SWING = true,
}

function M.findBatForAimbot()
    local char = player.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    for _, tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if tool:IsA("Tool") then
            local n = tool.Name:lower()
            if n:find("bat") or n:find("slap") then return tool end
        end
    end
    local bp = player:FindFirstChild("Backpack") or player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    if bp then
        for _, tool in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
            if tool:IsA("Tool") then
                local n = tool.Name:lower()
                if n:find("bat") or n:find("slap") then return tool end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

function M.getClosestTargetAimbot()
    local root = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return nil end
    local closest, minDist = nil, math.huge
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= player and plr.Character then
            local tRoot = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if tRoot and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local dist = (tRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if dist < minDist then minDist = dist; closest = tRoot end
            end
        end
    end
    return closest
end

function M.getClosestPlayerAimbot()
    local root = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return nil end
    local closest, minDist = nil, math.huge
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= player and plr.Character then
            local tRoot = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if tRoot and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local dist = (tRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if dist < minDist then minDist = dist; closest = plr end
            end
        end
    end
    return closest
end

function M.getNormalAimbotSpeed()
    if M.laggerModeEnabled or M.laggerCarryActive then
        return tonumber(M.LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED) or 40
    end
    return tonumber(M.AIMBOT_SPEED) or 58
end

function M.getAntiBypassAimbotSpeed()
    if M.laggerModeEnabled or M.laggerCarryActive then
        return tonumber(M.antiBypassLaggerAimbotSpeed) or 40
    end
    return tonumber(M.antiBypassAimbotSpeed) or 58
end

function M.getActiveAimbotSpeed()
    if M.aimbotMode == "Anti Bypass" then
        return M.getAntiBypassAimbotSpeed()
    end
    return M.getNormalAimbotSpeed()
end

local function _axeAimbotDestroyLV()
    pcall(function() if M._aimbotLV then M._aimbotLV:Destroy() end end)
    pcall(function() if M._aimbotAtt then M._aimbotAtt:Destroy() end end)
    M._aimbotLV, M._aimbotAtt = nil, nil
end

local function _axeAimbotEnsureLV(root)
    if not root or not root.Parent then return nil end
    if M._aimbotLV and M._aimbotLV.Parent == root and M._aimbotAtt and M._aimbotAtt.Parent == root then
        M._aimbotLV.Enabled = true
        return M._aimbotLV
    end
    _axeAimbotDestroyLV()
    local att = Instance.new("Attachment")
    att.Name = "FlowAdaptAimbotAtt"
    att.Parent = root
    local lv = Instance.new("LinearVelocity")
    lv.Name = "FlowAdaptAimbotLV"
    lv.Attachment0 = att
    lv.RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
    pcall(function() lv.ForceLimitMode = Enum.ForceLimitMode.Magnitude end)
    lv.MaxForce = math.huge
    lv.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
    lv.Enabled = true
    lv.Parent = root
    M._aimbotAtt = att
    M._aimbotLV = lv
    return lv
end

local function _axeAimbotReleaseLV()
    if M._aimbotLV and M._aimbotLV.Parent then
        M._aimbotLV.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
        M._aimbotLV.Enabled = false
    end
end

function M.swingCurrentBatAimbot(char)
    char = char or player.Character
    local bat = M.findBatForAimbot()
    if not bat then return end
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum and bat.Parent ~= char then
        pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end)
    end
    pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
    local ev = bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
    if ev then pcall(function() ev:FireServer() end) end
end

local function _stopAimbotExclusivePaths()
    if M.autoLeftEnabled then
        M.autoLeftEnabled = false
        pcall(function() if M.stopAutoLeft then M.stopAutoLeft() end end)
        if M.autoLeftSetVisual then M.autoLeftSetVisual(false) end
        if M.mobBtnRefs and M.mobBtnRefs.autoLeft then M.mobBtnRefs.autoLeft(false) end
    end
    if M.autoRightEnabled then
        M.autoRightEnabled = false
        pcall(function() if M.stopAutoRight then M.stopAutoRight() end end)
        if M.autoRightSetVisual then M.autoRightSetVisual(false) end
        if M.mobBtnRefs and M.mobBtnRefs.autoRight then M.mobBtnRefs.autoRight(false) end
    end
end

-- ---- NORMAL (Adapt LinearVelocity) ----
function M.startNormalAimbot()
    if M.aimbotConn then pcall(function() M.aimbotConn:Disconnect() end); M.aimbotConn = nil end
    _stopAimbotExclusivePaths()
    if M.bypassAimbotEnabled then M.stopBypassAimbot() end
    _axeAimbotDestroyLV()

    M._autoTPWasEnabledForBat = false
    if M.autoTPEnabled then
        M._autoTPWasEnabledForBat = true
        if M.stopAutoTP then M.stopAutoTP() end
        if M.setAutoTPVisual then M.setAutoTPVisual(false) end
    end

    M.autoBatEnabled = true
    M.aimbotMode = "Normal"
    M._aimbotTarget = nil
    M._aimbotLastScan = 0
    M._aimbotSwingCooldown = false

    local hum0 = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum0 then hum0.AutoRotate = false end

    -- Height offset: sit slightly above target HRP so Normal tracks elevated Y
    local NORMAL_HEIGHT = 3.2
    local NORMAL_VERT_GAIN = 6.5
    local NORMAL_VERT_MAX = 52

    M.aimbotConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not M.autoBatEnabled or M.aimbotMode ~= "Normal" then return end
        local char = player.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not char or not hum or not root or hum.Health <= 0 then
            _axeAimbotReleaseLV()
            return
        end

        if not char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
            local bat = M.findBatForAimbot()
            if bat then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end) end
        end

        local now = tick()
        local target = M._aimbotTarget
        if now - (M._aimbotLastScan or 0) > 0.1 or not target or not target.Parent then
            M._aimbotLastScan = now
            target = M.getClosestTargetAimbot()
            M._aimbotTarget = target
        end
        if not target then
            _axeAimbotReleaseLV()
            return
        end

        local speed = M.getNormalAimbotSpeed()
        -- Aim point: slightly above target (Y elevated)
        local aimPos = target.Position + Vector3.new(0, NORMAL_HEIGHT, 0)
        local toAim = aimPos - root.Position
        local flat = Vector3.new(toAim.X, 0, toAim.Z)
        local distFlat = flat.Magnitude

        local lv = _axeAimbotEnsureLV(root)
        if lv then
            local vx, vz = 0, 0
            if distFlat > 0.08 then
                local unit = flat.Unit
                vx = unit.X * speed
                vz = unit.Z * speed
            end
            -- Vertical correction toward elevated Y (clamped)
            local vy = math.clamp(toAim.Y * NORMAL_VERT_GAIN, -NORMAL_VERT_MAX, NORMAL_VERT_MAX)
            lv.VectorVelocity = Vector3.new(vx, vy, vz)
        end
        pcall(function()
            hum.AutoRotate = false
            if distFlat > 0.05 then
                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position, Vector3.new(target.Position.X, root.Position.Y, target.Position.Z))
            end
        end)

        if (target.Position - root.Position).Magnitude < 14 and not M._aimbotSwingCooldown then
            M._aimbotSwingCooldown = true
            M.swingCurrentBatAimbot(char)
            task.delay(0.12, function() M._aimbotSwingCooldown = false end)
        end
    end)

    if M.autoBatSetVisual then M.autoBatSetVisual(true) end
    if M.mobBtnRefs and M.mobBtnRefs.autoBat then M.mobBtnRefs.autoBat(true) end
end

-- ---- ANTI BYPASS (Adapt angular + stand offset) ----
function M.startAntiBypassAimbot()
    -- Full teardown so second enable always gets a fresh Heartbeat + clean velocity
    if M.aimbotConn then pcall(function() M.aimbotConn:Disconnect() end); M.aimbotConn = nil end
    _stopAimbotExclusivePaths()
    if M.bypassAimbotEnabled then M.stopBypassAimbot() end
    _axeAimbotDestroyLV()

    M._autoTPWasEnabledForBat = false
    if M.autoTPEnabled then
        M._autoTPWasEnabledForBat = true
        if M.stopAutoTP then M.stopAutoTP() end
        if M.setAutoTPVisual then M.setAutoTPVisual(false) end
    end

    M.autoBatEnabled = true
    M.aimbotMode = "Anti Bypass"
    M._aimbotSwingCooldown = false
    M._aimbotTarget = nil
    M._aimbotLastScan = 0

    local hum0 = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum0 then hum0.AutoRotate = false end
    local root0 = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if root0 then
        pcall(function()
            root0.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
    end

    local S = ADAPT_AB_SETTINGS
    M.aimbotConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not M.autoBatEnabled or M.aimbotMode ~= "Anti Bypass" then return end
        local char = player.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not char or not hum or not root or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

        hum.AutoRotate = false
        if not char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
            local bat = M.findBatForAimbot()
            if bat then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end) end
        end

        local target = M.getClosestTargetAimbot()
        if not target then
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            return
        end

        local aimTargetPos = target.Position + Vector3.new(0, S.V_OFFSET, 0)
        local look = aimTargetPos - root.Position
        local flatLook = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
        if flatLook.Magnitude > 0.01 then
            local targetYaw = math.deg(math.atan2(-flatLook.X, -flatLook.Z))
            local yawDelta = (targetYaw - root.Orientation.Y + 180) % 360 - 180
            local yawRate = math.clamp(yawDelta * 8, -S.MAX_TURN_RATE, S.MAX_TURN_RATE)
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, yawRate, 0)
        else
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end

        local speed = M.getAntiBypassAimbotSpeed()
        -- NaN-safe flat direction for stand offset
        local flatDir = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
        local standPos
        if flatDir.Magnitude > 0.05 then
            flatDir = flatDir.Unit
            standPos = target.Position + Vector3.new(0, S.HEIGHT, 0) + (flatDir * S.DISTANCE)
        else
            standPos = target.Position + Vector3.new(0, S.HEIGHT, 0)
        end
        local toStand = standPos - root.Position
        local push = Vector3.new(toStand.X, 0, toStand.Z)
        if push.Magnitude > 0.05 then
            push = push.Unit * speed
        else
            push = Vector3.zero
        end
        local vert = math.clamp(toStand.Y * 4, -S.VERT_SPEED, S.VERT_SPEED)
        -- Always write a finite velocity (never leave stale NaN)
        if push.X == push.X and vert == vert then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(push.X, vert, push.Z)
        end
        if flatDir.Magnitude > 0.05 or Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z).Magnitude > 0.05 then
            pcall(function()
                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position, Vector3.new(target.Position.X, root.Position.Y, target.Position.Z))
            end)
        end

        if S.AUTO_SWING and (target.Position - root.Position).Magnitude < 14 and not M._aimbotSwingCooldown then
            M._aimbotSwingCooldown = true
            M.swingCurrentBatAimbot(char)
            task.delay(0.3, function() M._aimbotSwingCooldown = false end)
        end
    end)

    if M.autoBatSetVisual then M.autoBatSetVisual(true) end
    if M.mobBtnRefs and M.mobBtnRefs.autoBat then M.mobBtnRefs.autoBat(true) end
end

function M.startBatAimbot()
    if M.aimbotMode == "Anti Bypass" then
        M.startAntiBypassAimbot()
    else
        M.startNormalAimbot()
    end
end

function M.stopBatAimbot()
    M.autoBatEnabled = false
    if M.aimbotConn then pcall(function() M.aimbotConn:Disconnect() end); M.aimbotConn = nil end
    _axeAimbotDestroyLV()
    M._aimbotTarget = nil
    M._aimbotSwingCooldown = false
    local hum = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then hum.AutoRotate = true end
    local root = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if root then root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end
    if M._autoTPWasEnabledForBat and M.startAutoTP then
        M._autoTPWasEnabledForBat = false
        M.startAutoTP()
        if M.setAutoTPVisual then M.setAutoTPVisual(true) end
    end
    if M.autoBatSetVisual then M.autoBatSetVisual(false) end
    if M.mobBtnRefs and M.mobBtnRefs.autoBat then M.mobBtnRefs.autoBat(false) end
end

function M.toggleBatAimbot()
    if M.autoBatEnabled then M.stopBatAimbot() else M.startBatAimbot() end
    return M.autoBatEnabled
end

function M.queueAutoBatStart()
    task.defer(function()
        if M.autoBatEnabled then
            if M.autoBatSetVisual then M.autoBatSetVisual(true) end
            if M.mobBtnRefs and M.mobBtnRefs.autoBat then M.mobBtnRefs.autoBat(true) end
            return
        end
        _stopAimbotExclusivePaths()
        if M.bypassAimbotEnabled then M.stopBypassAimbot() end
        M.startBatAimbot()
    end)
end


-- Body Lock suspend/restore for combat exclusivity (TP Bat / Aimbot)
M._bodyLockWasEnabled = M._bodyLockWasEnabled or false
function M.isCombatAimActive()
    return M.autoBatEnabled == true or M.bypassAimbotEnabled == true
end
function M.suspendBodyLockForCombat()
    if M._bodyLockEnabled then
        M._bodyLockWasEnabled = true
        if M.stopBodyLock then M.stopBodyLock() end
    end
end
function M.tryRestoreBodyLockAfterCombat()
    if M.isCombatAimActive and M.isCombatAimActive() then return end
    if M._bodyLockWasEnabled then
        M._bodyLockWasEnabled = false
        if M.startBodyLock then M.startBodyLock() end
    end
end

-- ---- TP BAT (blody.vs VisionBat motor — Y/X hysteresis + desync rep root) ----
-- Substitui o Anti-Desync Adapt do Flow. API bypassAimbot* preservada (UI/mobile/keybind).
function M._tpBatStopAutoPaths()
    -- exclusivity: stop normal/anti-bypass aimbot paths when TP Bat engages
    if M.stopBatAimbot then pcall(function() M.stopBatAimbot() end) end
    if type(_stopAimbotExclusivePaths) == "function" then pcall(_stopAimbotExclusivePaths) end
end

-- ============================================================
-- ============================================================
-- BAT TP (Rainy Hub Bat Desync TP - integrated)
-- ============================================================
M._batDesyncConn = nil
M._batDesyncHitCD = false


-- ============================================================
-- TP BAT (motor unico portado do VisionBat - limite de altura Y + desync de rep root)
-- Mantem os nomes bypassAimbot* para continuar ligado a UI,
-- aos botoes mobile e aos keybinds existentes do blody.vs.
-- ============================================================
M.TPBAT_SLAP_LIST = {
    "Bat","Slap","Iron Slap","Gold Slap","Diamond Slap",
    "Emerald Slap","Ruby Slap","Dark Matter Slap","Flame Slap",
    "Nuclear Slap","Galaxy Slap","Glitched Slap"
}

M.TPBAT = M.TPBAT or {}
TPBAT = M.TPBAT
TPBAT.version       = tonumber(M.tpBatVersion) or 1
TPBAT.SWING_CD      = 0.08
TPBAT.TP_DIST       = 8
TPBAT.HEIGHT_OFF    = 0.9
TPBAT.MAX_Y_DIFF    = 100 -- diferenca maxima de altura para seguir/atacar
TPBAT.RETURN_Y_DIFF = 80  -- volta a atacar abaixo disso (histerese)
TPBAT.MAX_X_DIFF    = 90  -- diferenca maxima no eixo X (pausa TP Bat se alvo longe no X)
TPBAT.RETURN_X_DIFF = 70  -- volta a atacar abaixo disso no X (histerese)
TPBAT.yBlocked      = false
TPBAT.xBlocked      = false
TPBAT.conn          = nil
TPBAT.renderConn    = nil
TPBAT.HRP           = nil
TPBAT.Hum           = nil

function M._tpbatSave()
    if type(M.saveConfig) == "function" then pcall(M.saveConfig) end
end

-- Deteccao de bat unificada (lista completa de slaps)
function M._bypassFindBat()
    local char = player.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    for _, name in ipairs(M.TPBAT_SLAP_LIST) do
        local t = char:FindFirstChild(name)
        if t and t:IsA("Tool") then return t end
    end
    local bp = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    if bp then
        for _, name in ipairs(M.TPBAT_SLAP_LIST) do
            local t = bp:FindFirstChild(name)
            if t and t:IsA("Tool") then
                local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(t) end) end
                return t
            end
        end
    end
    for _, ch in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if ch:IsA("Tool") and (ch.Name:lower():find("bat") or ch.Name:lower():find("slap")) then
            return ch
        end
    end
    return nil
end

function TPBAT.refreshChar(char)
    char = char or player.Character
    if not char then return end
    TPBAT.Hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    TPBAT.HRP = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.wait(0.2)
    TPBAT.refreshChar(char)
end)
if player.Character then
    task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.2); TPBAT.refreshChar(player.Character) end)
end

function TPBAT.getBat() return M._bypassFindBat() end

-- ============================================================
-- DETECCAO DE "ANTI BAT TP" REMOVIDA POR COMPLETO
-- O TP Bat agora segue qualquer alvo, mesmo com Anti Bat TP ligado.
-- ============================================================
M.ANTIBAT_DETECT_Y      = -400
M.ANTIBAT_DETECT_DESYNC = 30
M.ANTIBAT_DETECT_JUMP   = 120
TPBAT.currentTargetPlayer = nil
TPBAT.targetSamples = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})

function M.isPlayerAntiBatTP(plr)
    return false  -- deteccao removida
end

function TPBAT.guardAntiBat()
    return true  -- nunca bloqueia
end

function M.tpBatTargetAllowed(plr)
    return plr and plr.Character and true or false
end

function TPBAT.autoDisableByAntiBat()
    -- funcao esvaziada: nao desliga mais o TP Bat
end

function TPBAT.releaseRep()
    local root = TPBAT.HRP
    if not (root and root.Parent) then
        local c = player.Character
        root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end
    if root and sethiddenproperty then
        pcall(function()
            if M.antiBatTPEnabled and M._antiBatFake then
                sethiddenproperty(root, "PhysicsRepRootPart", M._antiBatFake)
            else
                sethiddenproperty(root, "PhysicsRepRootPart", root)
            end
        end)
    end
end

-- posicao segura? (bloqueia roots falsos / fora do mapa)
function TPBAT.safeTargetPos(part)
    if not (part and part.Parent) then return nil end
    if part.Position.Y <= M.ANTIBAT_DETECT_Y then return nil end
    local root = TPBAT.HRP
    if root and (root.Position - part.Position).Magnitude > 2000 then return nil end
    return part.Position
end

-- Alvo mais proximo com limite de altura + eixo X (histerese; ignora players mortos)
function TPBAT.getClosest()
    local root = TPBAT.HRP
    if not (root and root.Parent) then
        local char = player.Character
        root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        TPBAT.HRP = root
    end
    if not root then return nil end
    local yLimit = TPBAT.yBlocked and TPBAT.RETURN_Y_DIFF or TPBAT.MAX_Y_DIFF
    local xLimit = TPBAT.xBlocked and TPBAT.RETURN_X_DIFF or TPBAT.MAX_X_DIFF
    local cp, cd = nil, math.huge
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= player and p.Character then
            local tr = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local ph = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if tr and (not ph or ph.Health > 0) then
                local yDiff = math.abs(tr.Position.Y - root.Position.Y)
                local xDiff = math.abs(tr.Position.X - root.Position.X)
                if yDiff <= yLimit and xDiff <= xLimit then
                    local d = (root.Position - tr.Position).Magnitude
                    if d < cd then cd = d; cp = p; TPBAT.currentTargetPlayer = p end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return cp
end
M._bypassGetClosest = TPBAT.getClosest

-- Swing unificado: equipa se preciso, ativa a tool e dispara os remotes
function TPBAT.tryHit(cd)
    if M.bypassHitCD then return end
    M.bypassHitCD = true
    pcall(function()
        local char = player.Character
        local bat = TPBAT.getBat()
        if bat then
            if char and bat.Parent ~= char then
                local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end) end
            end
            pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
            local ev = bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
            if ev then pcall(function() ev:FireServer() end) end
            local rf = bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteFunction")
            if rf then pcall(function() rf:InvokeServer() end) end
        end
    end)
    task.delay(cd or TPBAT.SWING_CD, function() M.bypassHitCD = false end)
end

function TPBAT.clearConns()
    if TPBAT.conn then pcall(function() TPBAT.conn:Disconnect() end); TPBAT.conn = nil end
    if TPBAT.renderConn then pcall(function() TPBAT.renderConn:Disconnect() end); TPBAT.renderConn = nil end
    M._batDesyncConn = nil
    M.bypassAimbotConn = nil
end

-- ---------- V1 VisionBat (limite Y/X + desync rep root) ----------
function TPBAT.startV1()
    TPBAT.clearConns()
    M.bypassHitCD = false
    TPBAT.yBlocked = false
    TPBAT.xBlocked = false
    TPBAT._wasBlocked = false
    TPBAT.refreshChar(player.Character)
    -- stop residual velocity/speed objects so server does not reconcile us back
    pcall(function() if M.destroySpeedObjects then M.destroySpeedObjects() end end)
    if TPBAT.HRP then
        TPBAT.HRP.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        TPBAT.HRP.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end

    TPBAT.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not M.bypassAimbotEnabled then return end
        if not TPBAT.HRP or not TPBAT.HRP.Parent or not TPBAT.Hum or not TPBAT.Hum.Parent then
            TPBAT.refreshChar(player.Character)
            if not (TPBAT.HRP and TPBAT.Hum) then return end
        end
        local hrp = TPBAT.HRP
        local tgt = TPBAT.getClosest()
        if not (tgt and tgt.Character) then
            if not TPBAT._wasBlocked then
                TPBAT._wasBlocked = true
                TPBAT.releaseRep()
            end
            TPBAT.yBlocked = true
            return
        end
        local tr = tgt.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not tr then return end

        -- limite de altura com histerese (VisionBat)
        local yDiff = math.abs(tr.Position.Y - hrp.Position.Y)
        local yLimit = TPBAT.yBlocked and TPBAT.RETURN_Y_DIFF or TPBAT.MAX_Y_DIFF
        if yDiff > yLimit then
            if not TPBAT.yBlocked then
                TPBAT.yBlocked = true
                TPBAT._wasBlocked = true
                TPBAT.releaseRep()
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
            return
        end
        TPBAT.yBlocked = false

        -- limite no eixo X com histerese (pausa sem spam de releaseRep)
        local xDiff = math.abs(tr.Position.X - hrp.Position.X)
        local xLimit = TPBAT.xBlocked and TPBAT.RETURN_X_DIFF or TPBAT.MAX_X_DIFF
        if xDiff > xLimit then
            if not TPBAT.xBlocked then
                TPBAT.xBlocked = true
                TPBAT._wasBlocked = true
                TPBAT.releaseRep()
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
            return
        end
        TPBAT.xBlocked = false
        TPBAT._wasBlocked = false

        local base = TPBAT.safeTargetPos(tr)
        if not base then
            if not TPBAT._wasBlocked then
                TPBAT._wasBlocked = true
                TPBAT.releaseRep()
            end
            return
        end

        -- desync: vincula o rep root ao alvo (VisionBat)
        if sethiddenproperty then
            pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(hrp, "PhysicsRepRootPart", tr) end)
        end

        local tPos = base + Vector3.new(0, TPBAT.HEIGHT_OFF, 0)
        -- lock position every frame while active (not only when > TP_DIST) to defeat server snap-back
        local look = Vector3.new(tr.Position.X, tPos.Y, tr.Position.Z)
        if (look - tPos).Magnitude < 0.05 then
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(tPos)
        else
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(tPos, look)
        end
        -- kill residual velocity so physics/speed cannot pull us back to previous pos
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        pcall(function()
            hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)

        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam then cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position, tr.Position) end

        TPBAT.tryHit()
    end)
    M._batDesyncConn = TPBAT.conn
    M.bypassAimbotConn = TPBAT.conn
end


-- ---------- V2 Blod classic (RenderStepped chase + TP loop desync) ----------
-- Portado do TP Bat V3 do Blod hub; exposto como V2 no Flow.
function TPBAT.getClosestRootV2()
    local root = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return nil, math.huge end
    local closest, minDist = nil, math.huge
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= player and plr.Character then
            local tRoot = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if tRoot and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local dist = (tRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if dist < minDist then
                    minDist = dist
                    closest = tRoot
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closest, minDist
end

function TPBAT.startV2()
    TPBAT.clearConns()
    M.bypassHitCD = false
    TPBAT.refreshChar(player.Character)

    local hum0 = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum0 then
        if M._tpBatPrevAutoRotate == nil then
            M._tpBatPrevAutoRotate = hum0.AutoRotate
        end
        hum0.AutoRotate = false
    end

    -- Chase loop (RenderStepped) — Blod V3 motor
    TPBAT.renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not M.bypassAimbotEnabled or (tonumber(TPBAT.version) or 1) ~= 2 then return end
        local char = player.Character
        if not char then return end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not root or not hum then return end

        if not char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
            local bat = TPBAT.getBat and TPBAT.getBat() or M._bypassFindBat and M._bypassFindBat()
            if bat then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end) end
        end

        local target, targetDist = TPBAT.getClosestRootV2()
        if not target then return end

        local myPos = root.Position
        local targetPos = target.Position
        local direction = targetPos - myPos
        local flatDir = Vector3.new(direction.X, 0, direction.Z)
        if flatDir.Magnitude > 0 then
            flatDir = flatDir.Unit
        else
            flatDir = Vector3.zero
        end

        local chaseSpeed = 58
        local desiredHeight = targetPos.Y + 3.7
        local yVel = (desiredHeight - myPos.Y) * 19.5
        if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
            yVel = math.max(yVel, 13)
        end
        yVel = math.clamp(yVel, -70, 110)

        local desiredVel = Vector3.new(flatDir.X * chaseSpeed, yVel, flatDir.Z * chaseSpeed)
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity:Lerp(desiredVel, 0.8)

        local toTarget = targetPos - myPos
        if toTarget.Magnitude > 0.1 then
            local goalCF = CFrame.lookAt(myPos, targetPos)
            local diffCF = root.CFrame:Inverse() * goalCF
            local rx, ry, rz = diffCF:ToEulerAnglesXYZ()
            rx = math.clamp(rx, -2.5, 2.5)
            ry = math.clamp(ry, -2.5, 2.5)
            rz = math.clamp(rz, -2.5, 2.5)
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = root.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(rx * 42, ry * 42, rz * 42))
        end

        local hitDist = tonumber(M.bypassHitDist) or 8
        if targetDist <= hitDist then
            TPBAT.tryHit(0.35)
        end
    end)

    -- TP + camera + PhysicsRepRootPart loop
    TPBAT._v2Token = (TPBAT._v2Token or 0) + 1
    local token = TPBAT._v2Token
    task.spawn(function()
        while M.bypassAimbotEnabled and (tonumber(TPBAT.version) or 1) == 2 and token == TPBAT._v2Token do
            task.wait(0.05)
            if not M.bypassAimbotEnabled or (tonumber(TPBAT.version) or 1) ~= 2 then break end
            local target = select(1, TPBAT.getClosestRootV2())
            if not target then
                -- no-op frame
            else
                local char = player.Character
                local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrp then
                    local targetPos = target.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.9, 0)
                    if (hrp.Position - targetPos).Magnitude > 8 then
                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos)
                    end
                    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                    if cam then
                        cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position, target.Position)
                    end
                    if sethiddenproperty then
                        pcall(function()
                            sethiddenproperty(hrp, "PhysicsRepRootPart", target)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    M.bypassAimbotConn = TPBAT.renderConn
    M._batDesyncConn = TPBAT.renderConn
end

function TPBAT.start()
    local ver = tonumber(TPBAT.version) or tonumber(M.tpBatVersion) or 1
    if ver == 2 then
        TPBAT.startV2()
    else
        TPBAT.startV1()
    end
end

function TPBAT.stopEngines()
    TPBAT.clearConns()
    M.bypassHitCD = false
    M._batDesyncHitCD = false
    TPBAT.yBlocked = false
    TPBAT.xBlocked = false
    TPBAT._wasBlocked = false
    local c = player.Character
    local root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if root then
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        if sethiddenproperty then pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(root, "PhysicsRepRootPart", root) end) end
    end
    local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        if M._tpBatPrevAutoRotate ~= nil then
            hum.AutoRotate = M._tpBatPrevAutoRotate
            M._tpBatPrevAutoRotate = nil
        else
            hum.AutoRotate = true
        end
        hum.PlatformStand = false
        pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end)
    end
    -- invalidate V2 background token so the TP loop exits
    TPBAT._v2Token = (TPBAT._v2Token or 0) + 1
end

-- keep godmode stubs so any old calls don't error
function M._bypassClearGodConns() end
function M._bypassProtectCharacter(char) end
function M.enableBypassGodmode() end
function M.disableBypassGodmode() end

function M.startBypassAimbot()
    if M.autoBatEnabled then M.stopBatAimbot() end
    -- Body Lock cannot run alongside TP Bat (remember preference for restore)
    M.suspendBodyLockForCombat()
    -- Force clean stop first so second enable always rebuilds engines
    M.bypassAimbotEnabled = false
    TPBAT.stopEngines()
    M.bypassHitCD = false
    M._batDesyncHitCD = false
    local ver = tonumber(M.tpBatVersion) or 1
    if ver ~= 2 then ver = 1 end
    TPBAT.version = ver
    M.tpBatVersion = ver
    TPBAT.refreshChar(player.Character)
    M.bypassAimbotEnabled = true
    TPBAT.start()
    if M.setBypassVisual then M.setBypassVisual(true) end
    if M.mobBtnRefs.bypass then M.mobBtnRefs.bypass(true) end
end

function M.stopBypassAimbot()
    M.bypassAimbotEnabled = false
    TPBAT.stopEngines()
    M._bypassTarget = nil
    TPBAT.currentTargetPlayer = nil
    if M.setBypassVisual then M.setBypassVisual(false) end
    if M.mobBtnRefs.bypass then M.mobBtnRefs.bypass(false) end

    -- restore Body Lock only when Aimbot AND TP Bat are both off
    task.defer(function()
        pcall(function() M.tryRestoreBodyLockAfterCombat() end)
    end)
end

function M.toggleBypassAimbot()
    if M.bypassAimbotEnabled then
        M.stopBypassAimbot()
    else
        M.startBypassAimbot()
    end
    if M.setBypassVisual then
        M.setBypassVisual(M.bypassAimbotEnabled)
    end
    if M.mobBtnRefs.bypass then
        M.mobBtnRefs.bypass(M.bypassAimbotEnabled)
    end
    M._tpbatSave()
    return M.bypassAimbotEnabled
end

function M.setTPBatVersion(v)
    local n = tonumber(v) or 1
    if n ~= 2 then n = 1 end
    M.tpBatVersion = n
    TPBAT.version = n
    if M.setTPBatVersionUI then
        pcall(function() M.setTPBatVersionUI(n == 2 and "V2" or "V1") end)
    end
    -- live switch: restart engine if TP Bat is currently on
    if M.bypassAimbotEnabled then
        TPBAT.stopEngines()
        TPBAT.start()
    end
    return n
end


function M.doAutoTPDown(force)
    local char=player.Character;if not char then return end;local hrp=char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart");if not hrp then return end
    local hum2=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid");if not hum2 then return end
    if not force then if hum2.FloorMaterial~=Enum.Material.Air then return end;if not(hrp.Position.Y>=M.autoTPHeight) then return end end
    hrp.CFrame=CFrame.new(hrp.Position.X,-7.00,hrp.Position.Z)*CFrame.Angles(0,select(2,hrp.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()),0);hrp.Velocity=Vector3.zero
end

function M.startAutoTP()
    if M.autoTPConn then task.cancel(M.autoTPConn);M.autoTPConn=nil end
    M.autoTPConn=task.spawn(function() while M.autoTPEnabled do task.wait(0.1);pcall(function() M.doAutoTPDown(false) end) end end)
end

function M.stopAutoTP() M.autoTPEnabled=false;if M.autoTPConn then task.cancel(M.autoTPConn);M.autoTPConn=nil end end

function M.runTPFloor() pcall(function() M.doAutoTPDown(true) end) end

function M.enableStretchRez()
    M.stretchRezEnabled=true;if M.stretchRezConn then M.stretchRezConn:Disconnect() end
    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("Movee_Stretch") end)
    pcall(function() RunService:BindToRenderStep("Movee_Stretch",Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value-1,function() local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera;if cam then cam.CFrame=cam.CFrame*CFrame.new(0,0,0,1,0,0,0,0.8,0,0,0,1) end end) end)
end

function M.disableStretchRez() M.stretchRezEnabled=false;pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("Movee_Stretch") end) end

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- ANTI SUMMER BASE (ONLY remove blocking Anchor parts — never wipe bases)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
function M.isSummerBaseName(name)
    if not name then return false end
    local n = tostring(name):lower()
    -- strict: only explicit summer base names (not beach/palm/prop — those kill enemy bases)
    return n == "summerbase"
        or n == "summer_base"
        or n:find("summerbase", 1, true) ~= nil
        or n:find("summer_base", 1, true) ~= nil
end

function M.isAnchorName(name)
    if not name then return false end
    local n = tostring(name):lower()
    return n == "anchor" or n == "anchors"
end

function M.stripBlockingAnchor(obj)
    if not obj or not obj.Parent then return end
    local key = tostring(obj:GetFullName())
    if M._antiSummerCleaned[key] then return end
    M._antiSummerCleaned[key] = true
    pcall(function()
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("MeshPart") then
            obj.CanCollide = false
            obj.CanQuery = false
            obj.CanTouch = false
            obj.Transparency = 1
        end
        obj:Destroy()
    end)
end

function M.cleanSummerBaseAnchors()
    if not M.antiSummerBaseEnabled then return end
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then return end

    -- Only scan Plots (not whole workspace — was causing lag + wiping bases)
    for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        local isSummer = M.isSummerBaseName(plot.Name)
        if not isSummer then
            for _, d in ipairs(plot:GetDescendants()) do
                if M.isSummerBaseName(d.Name) then
                    isSummer = true
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if isSummer then
            -- ONLY strip objects literally named Anchor / Anchors
            for _, d in ipairs(plot:GetDescendants()) do
                if M.isAnchorName(d.Name) then
                    M.stripBlockingAnchor(d)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function M.enableAntiSummerBase()
    M.antiSummerBaseEnabled = true
    M._antiSummerCleaned = {}
    M.cleanSummerBaseAnchors()
    if M.antiSummerBaseConn then
        pcall(function() M.antiSummerBaseConn:Disconnect() end)
        M.antiSummerBaseConn = nil
    end
    M.antiSummerBaseConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if not M.antiSummerBaseEnabled then return end
        if not M.isAnchorName(obj.Name) then return end
        task.defer(function()
            if not M.antiSummerBaseEnabled or not obj.Parent then return end
            -- only if under Plots and near a summer-named container
            local p = obj
            local underPlots, nearSummer = false, false
            while p and p ~= workspace do
                if p.Name == "Plots" or (p.Parent and p.Parent.Name == "Plots") then underPlots = true end
                if M.isSummerBaseName(p.Name) then nearSummer = true end
                p = p.Parent
            end
            if underPlots and nearSummer then
                M.stripBlockingAnchor(obj)
            end
        end)
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while M.antiSummerBaseEnabled do
            M.cleanSummerBaseAnchors()
            task.wait(5) -- slower scan = less lag
        end
    end)
end

function M.disableAntiSummerBase()
    M.antiSummerBaseEnabled = false
    if M.antiSummerBaseConn then
        pcall(function() M.antiSummerBaseConn:Disconnect() end)
        M.antiSummerBaseConn = nil
    end
end

function M._isUnderPlots(obj)
    local p = obj
    while p and p ~= workspace do
        if p.Name == "Plots" then return true end
        p = p.Parent
    end
    return false
end

-- ============================================================
-- ANTI-LAG v2  (MAX FPS ENGINE)
-- • fila assíncrona (não trava o jogo ao ligar)
-- • guarda estado original de tudo pra restaurar 100% ao desligar
-- • nunca mexe nas bases (Plots) nem no personagem local
-- ============================================================
M._alStore = M._alStore or setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}) -- weak keys
M._alQueue = M._alQueue or {}
M._alWorker = M._alWorker or nil
M._alTerrainSaved = nil
M._alLightSaved = nil

local AL_FX = {
    ParticleEmitter = true, Trail = true, Beam = true, Fire = true,
    Smoke = true, Sparkles = true, Explosion = true,
}

function M._alSave(obj, key, val)
    local t = M._alStore[obj]
    if not t then t = {}; M._alStore[obj] = t end
    if t[key] == nil then t[key] = val end
end


-- ============================================================
-- ANTI LAG (portado do Blody)
-- ============================================================
function M.applyAntiLagDerender(obj)
    pcall(function()
        if obj:IsA("Accessory") or obj:IsA("Hat") then obj:Destroy()
        elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then obj.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic; obj.Reflectance = 0; obj.CastShadow = false
        elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then obj.Transparency = 1
        elseif obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or obj:IsA("Trail") or obj:IsA("Beam") or obj:IsA("Fire") or obj:IsA("Smoke") or obj:IsA("Sparkles") then obj.Enabled = false
        elseif obj:IsA("AnimationController") or obj:IsA("Animator") then
            for _, t in ipairs(obj:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do pcall(function() t:Stop(0) end) end
        end
    end)
end

function M.enableAntiLag()
    M.removeAccessoriesEnabled = true
    M.antiLagEnabled = true
    M.defLightBrightness = M.defLightBrightness or Lighting.Brightness
    M.defLightClock = M.defLightClock or Lighting.ClockTime
    M.defLightAmbient = M.defLightAmbient or Lighting.OutdoorAmbient
    Lighting.GlobalShadows = false; Lighting.FogEnd = 1e10; Lighting.Brightness = 1
    Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0; Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
    for _, e in pairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
        pcall(function()
            if e:IsA("BlurEffect") or e:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or e:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or e:IsA("BloomEffect") or e:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then e.Enabled = false end
        end)
    end
    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do M.applyAntiLagDerender(obj) end
    if M.antiLagDescConn then M.antiLagDescConn:Disconnect() end
    M.antiLagDescConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if M.removeAccessoriesEnabled then M.applyAntiLagDerender(obj) end
    end)
end

function M.disableAntiLag()
    M.removeAccessoriesEnabled = false
    M.antiLagEnabled = false
    if M.antiLagDescConn then M.antiLagDescConn:Disconnect(); M.antiLagDescConn = nil end
    pcall(function()
        if M.defLightBrightness then Lighting.Brightness = M.defLightBrightness end
        if M.defLightClock then Lighting.ClockTime = M.defLightClock end
        if M.defLightAmbient then Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = M.defLightAmbient end
        Lighting.ExposureCompensation = 0
    end)
end



-- ============================================================
-- ANTI-RAGDOLL
-- ============================================================
M.antiRagdollNoSplatterCooldown = 0

function M.forceNoSplatterReset()
    local char = player.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hum or not root or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

    pcall(function()
        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
        root.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        root.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

        for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("Motor6D") then obj.Enabled = true end
            if obj:IsA("Constraint") then obj.Enabled = true end
        end

        workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum

        local PM = player.PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
        if PM then
            local CM = require(PM:FindFirstChild("ControlModule"))
            if CM then CM:Enable() end
        end

        hum.AutoRotate = true
        hum.PlatformStand = false
        hum.Sit = false
    end)
end

function M.startAntiRagdoll()
    if M.Conns.antiRag then return end
    M.Conns.antiRag = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not M.antiRagdollEnabled then return end
        local char = player.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

        local state = hum:GetState()
        local ragdolled = (state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or
                          state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or
                          state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown)

        if M.antiRagdollMode == "No Splatter" then
            if ragdolled then
                local now = tick()
                if now - (M.antiRagdollNoSplatterCooldown or 0) > 0.15 then
                    M.antiRagdollNoSplatterCooldown = now
                    M.forceNoSplatterReset()
                end
            end
            return
        end

        if not root then return end
        local endTime = player:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")
        if endTime and (endTime - workspace:GetServerTimeNow()) > 0 then
            ragdolled = true
        end
        if ragdolled then
            pcall(function()
                player:SetAttribute("RagdollEndTime", workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
            end)
            for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or
                   (d:IsA("Attachment") and d.Name:find("RagdollAttachment")) then
                    d:Destroy()
                end
            end
            for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("Motor6D") and obj.Enabled == false then
                    obj.Enabled = true
                end
            end
            if hum.Health > 0 then
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
            end
            workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum
            root.Anchored = false
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
    end)
end

function M.stopAntiRagdoll()
    if M.Conns.antiRag then
        M.Conns.antiRag:Disconnect()
        M.Conns.antiRag = nil
    end
end



-- ============================================================
-- INFINITE JUMP (May Duels) — JumpRequest + Hold (Space / Jump)
-- ============================================================
M.infJumpEnabled = false
M.infJumpMode = "hold" -- May Duels is hold-style only
M.jumpHeld = false
M._ijJumpReqConn = nil
M._ijHoldConn = nil

local function _mayApplyInfJump(root)
    if not root then return end
    -- Prefer AssemblyLinearVelocity; fallback Velocity (May Duels original used Velocity)
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local v = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X, 55, v.Z)
    end)
    if not ok then
        pcall(function()
            root.Velocity = Vector3.new(root.Velocity.X, 55, root.Velocity.Z)
        end)
    end
end

local function _mayInfJumpY(root)
    local y = 0
    pcall(function() y = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y end)
    if y == 0 then
        pcall(function() y = root.Velocity.Y end)
    end
    return y
end

function M.stopManualInfJumpLoop()
    -- no-op alias (API compat)
end

function M.stopHoldInfJump()
    -- connections stay gated by M.infJumpEnabled
    M.jumpHeld = false
end

function M.startManualInfJumpLoop()
    M.infJumpEnabled = true
    M.infJumpMode = "hold"
end

function M.startHoldInfJump()
    M.infJumpEnabled = true
    M.infJumpMode = "hold"
end

function M.setInfJumpEnabled(on)
    M.infJumpEnabled = on and true or false
    if not M.infJumpEnabled then
        M.jumpHeld = false
    end
end

-- One-time connections (gated by flag — May Duels pattern, no leak)
if not M._ijJumpReqConn then
    M._ijJumpReqConn = UIS.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
        if not M.infJumpEnabled then return end
        local char = player.Character
        if not char then return end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root then _mayApplyInfJump(root) end
    end)
end

if not M._ijHoldConn then
    M._ijHoldConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not M.infJumpEnabled then return end
        local char = player.Character
        if not char then return end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local jumpHeld = UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) or (hum and hum.Jump == true) or M.jumpHeld
        if jumpHeld and _mayInfJumpY(root) < 30 then
            _mayApplyInfJump(root)
        end
    end)
end

-- Mobile JumpButton hold
task.spawn(function()
    local pg = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10)
    if not pg then return end
    local function hookBtn(btn)
        if btn:IsA("GuiButton") and btn.Name == "JumpButton" and not btn:GetAttribute("FlowMayIJHooked") then
            btn:SetAttribute("FlowMayIJHooked", true)
            btn.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
                if M.infJumpEnabled then M.jumpHeld = true end
            end)
            btn.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() M.jumpHeld = false end)
            btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() M.jumpHeld = false end)
        end
    end
    for _, d in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do hookBtn(d) end
    pg.DescendantAdded:Connect(hookBtn)
end)

function M.startUnwalk()
    local c=player.Character;if not c then return end;local hum=c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then for _,t in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do t:Stop() end end
    local anim=c:FindFirstChild("Animate");if anim then M.unwalkSavedAnimate=anim:Clone();anim:Destroy() end
end

function M.stopUnwalk() local c=player.Character;if c and M.unwalkSavedAnimate then M.unwalkSavedAnimate:Clone().Parent=c;M.unwalkSavedAnimate=nil end end


function M.hasBrainrotInHand()
    local char = player.Character
    if not char then return false end
    for _, item in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if item:IsA("Tool") then
            local name = item.Name:lower()
            if name:find("brainrot", 1, true) or name:find("skibidi", 1, true) or name:find("toilet", 1, true) then
                return true
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end

function M.forceLaggerCarryWhileHolding()
    if not M.hasBrainrotInHand() then return false end
    M.carrySpeedActive = false
    M.laggerModeEnabled = false
    M.laggerCarryActive = true
    return true
end

function M.toggleCarryMode()
    if M.forceLaggerCarryWhileHolding() then
        M.refreshSpeedModeLabel()
        if M.mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed then M.mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed(false) end
        if M.mobBtnRefs.lagger then M.mobBtnRefs.lagger(false) end
        if M.mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry then M.mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry(true) end
        if M.carryModeBtn then M.carryModeBtn.Text = "Carry Off" end
        if M.laggerModeBtn then M.laggerModeBtn.Text = "Lag Off" end
        if M.laggerCarryBtn then M.laggerCarryBtn.Text = "L.Carry On" end
        saveCherryConfig()
        return
    end
    M.carrySpeedActive = not M.carrySpeedActive
    if M.carrySpeedActive then
        M.laggerCarryActive = false
        M.laggerModeEnabled = false
    end
    M.refreshSpeedModeLabel()
    if M.mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed then M.mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed(M.carrySpeedActive) end
    if M.mobBtnRefs.lagger then M.mobBtnRefs.lagger(M.laggerModeEnabled) end
    if M.mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry then M.mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry(M.laggerCarryActive) end
    if M.carryModeBtn then
        M.carryModeBtn.Text = M.carrySpeedActive and "Carry On" or "Carry Off"
    end
    if M.laggerModeBtn then
        M.laggerModeBtn.Text = M.laggerModeEnabled and "Lag On" or "Lag Off"
    end
    if M.laggerCarryBtn then
        M.laggerCarryBtn.Text = M.laggerCarryActive and "L.Carry On" or "L.Carry Off"
    end
    saveCherryConfig()
end

function M.toggleLaggerMode()
    if M.forceLaggerCarryWhileHolding() then
        M.refreshSpeedModeLabel()
        if M.mobBtnRefs.lagger then M.mobBtnRefs.lagger(false) end
        if M.mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry then M.mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry(true) end
        if M.laggerModeBtn then M.laggerModeBtn.Text = "Lag Off" end
        if M.laggerCarryBtn then M.laggerCarryBtn.Text = "L.Carry On" end
        saveCherryConfig()
        return
    end
    M.laggerModeEnabled = not M.laggerModeEnabled
    if M.laggerModeEnabled then
        M.laggerCarryActive = false
        M.carrySpeedActive = false
    end
    M.refreshSpeedModeLabel()
    if M.mobBtnRefs.lagger then M.mobBtnRefs.lagger(M.laggerModeEnabled) end
    if M.mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed then M.mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed(M.carrySpeedActive) end
    if M.mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry then M.mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry(M.laggerCarryActive) end
    if M.carryModeBtn then
        M.carryModeBtn.Text = M.carrySpeedActive and "Carry On" or "Carry Off"
    end
    if M.laggerModeBtn then
        M.laggerModeBtn.Text = M.laggerModeEnabled and "Lag On" or "Lag Off"
    end
    if M.laggerCarryBtn then
        M.laggerCarryBtn.Text = M.laggerCarryActive and "L.Carry On" or "L.Carry Off"
    end
    saveCherryConfig()
end

function M.cycleLaggerModeBind()
    if M.forceLaggerCarryWhileHolding() then
        M.refreshSpeedModeLabel()
        if M.mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed then M.mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed(false) end
        if M.mobBtnRefs.lagger then M.mobBtnRefs.lagger(false) end
        if M.mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry then M.mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry(true) end
        if M.carryModeBtn then M.carryModeBtn.Text = "Carry Off" end
        if M.laggerModeBtn then M.laggerModeBtn.Text = "Lag Off" end
        if M.laggerCarryBtn then M.laggerCarryBtn.Text = "L.Carry On" end
        saveCherryConfig()
        return
    end
    if not M.laggerCarryActive and not M.laggerModeEnabled then
        M.laggerCarryActive = true
        M.laggerModeEnabled = false
        M.carrySpeedActive = false
    elseif M.laggerCarryActive then
        M.laggerCarryActive = false
        M.laggerModeEnabled = true
        M.carrySpeedActive = false
    else
        M.laggerModeEnabled = false
        M.laggerCarryActive = true
        M.carrySpeedActive = false
    end

    M.refreshSpeedModeLabel()
    if M.mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed then M.mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed(M.carrySpeedActive) end
    if M.mobBtnRefs.lagger then M.mobBtnRefs.lagger(M.laggerModeEnabled) end
    if M.mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry then M.mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry(M.laggerCarryActive) end
    if M.carryModeBtn then M.carryModeBtn.Text = M.carrySpeedActive and "Carry On" or "Carry Off" end
    if M.laggerModeBtn then M.laggerModeBtn.Text = M.laggerModeEnabled and "Lag On" or "Lag Off" end
    if M.laggerCarryBtn then M.laggerCarryBtn.Text = M.laggerCarryActive and "L.Carry On" or "L.Carry Off" end
    saveCherryConfig()
end

function M.toggleLaggerCarry()
    M.laggerCarryActive = not M.laggerCarryActive
    if M.laggerCarryActive then
        M.laggerModeEnabled = false
        M.carrySpeedActive = false
    end
    M.refreshSpeedModeLabel()
    if M.mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed then M.mobBtnRefs.carrySpeed(M.carrySpeedActive) end
    if M.mobBtnRefs.lagger then M.mobBtnRefs.lagger(M.laggerModeEnabled) end
    if M.mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry then M.mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry(M.laggerCarryActive) end
    if M.laggerModeBtn then
        M.laggerModeBtn.Text = M.laggerModeEnabled and "Lag On" or "Lag Off"
    end
    if M.carryModeBtn then
        M.carryModeBtn.Text = M.carrySpeedActive and "Carry On" or "Carry Off"
    end
    if M.laggerCarryBtn then
        M.laggerCarryBtn.Text = M.laggerCarryActive and "L.Carry On" or "L.Carry Off"
    end
    saveCherryConfig()
end

function M.stopAutoLeft()
    M.autoLeftEnabled = false
    if M.alConn then M.alConn:Disconnect(); M.alConn = nil end
    M.alPhase = 1
    local char = player.Character
    if char then
        local h = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if h then h:Move(Vector3.zero, false) end
    end
    if M.autoLeftSetVisual then M.autoLeftSetVisual(false) end
    if M.mobBtnRefs.autoLeft then M.mobBtnRefs.autoLeft(false) end
end

function M.stopAutoRight()
    M.autoRightEnabled = false
    if M.arConn then M.arConn:Disconnect(); M.arConn = nil end
    M.arPhase = 1
    local char = player.Character
    if char then
        local h = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if h then h:Move(Vector3.zero, false) end
    end
    if M.autoRightSetVisual then M.autoRightSetVisual(false) end
    if M.mobBtnRefs.autoRight then M.mobBtnRefs.autoRight(false) end
end

-- Original fixed-path Auto Left / Right (as before)
function M.startAutoLeft()
    -- Auto Left exclusao
    if M._stopTPBatForAutoPath then M._stopTPBatForAutoPath() end
    if M.alConn then M.alConn:Disconnect() end
    M.alPhase = 1
    M.autoLeftEnabled = true
    M.alConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not M.autoLeftEnabled then return end
        local char = player.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not hum then return end
        local spd = (M.getAutoPathSpeed and M.getAutoPathSpeed()) or (M.NS or 60)
        if M.alPhase == 1 then
            local tgt = Vector3.new(M.AP_L1.X, hrp.Position.Y, M.AP_L1.Z)
            if (tgt - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
                M.alPhase = 2
                local d = M.AP_L2 - hrp.Position
                local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z)
                if mv.Magnitude > 0.01 then mv = mv.Unit end
                hum:Move(mv, false)
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, mv.Z * spd)
                return
            end
            local d = M.AP_L1 - hrp.Position
            local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z)
            if mv.Magnitude > 0.01 then mv = mv.Unit end
            hum:Move(mv, false)
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, mv.Z * spd)
        elseif M.alPhase == 2 then
            local tgt = Vector3.new(M.AP_L2.X, hrp.Position.Y, M.AP_L2.Z)
            if (tgt - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
                hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                M.autoLeftEnabled = false
                if M.alConn then M.alConn:Disconnect(); M.alConn = nil end
                M.alPhase = 1
                if M.autoLeftSetVisual then M.autoLeftSetVisual(false) end
                if M.mobBtnRefs.autoLeft then M.mobBtnRefs.autoLeft(false) end
                return
            end
            local d = M.AP_L2 - hrp.Position
            local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z)
            if mv.Magnitude > 0.01 then mv = mv.Unit end
            hum:Move(mv, false)
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, mv.Z * spd)
        end
        if M.autoMoveSwingEnabled and not M._alSwingDebounce then
            M._alSwingDebounce = true
            local bat = M.findBat and M.findBat() or (M.findBatForAimbot and M.findBatForAimbot())
            if bat then
                if bat.Parent ~= char then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end) end
                pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
            end
            task.delay(M.autoMoveSwingInterval or 0.3, function() M._alSwingDebounce = false end)
        end
    end)
end

function M.startAutoRight()
    -- Auto Right exclusao
    if M._stopTPBatForAutoPath then M._stopTPBatForAutoPath() end
    if M.arConn then M.arConn:Disconnect() end
    M.arPhase = 1
    M.autoRightEnabled = true
    M.arConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not M.autoRightEnabled then return end
        local char = player.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not hum then return end
        local spd = (M.getAutoPathSpeed and M.getAutoPathSpeed()) or (M.NS or 60)
        if M.arPhase == 1 then
            local tgt = Vector3.new(M.AP_R1.X, hrp.Position.Y, M.AP_R1.Z)
            if (tgt - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
                M.arPhase = 2
                local d = M.AP_R2 - hrp.Position
                local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z)
                if mv.Magnitude > 0.01 then mv = mv.Unit end
                hum:Move(mv, false)
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, mv.Z * spd)
                return
            end
            local d = M.AP_R1 - hrp.Position
            local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z)
            if mv.Magnitude > 0.01 then mv = mv.Unit end
            hum:Move(mv, false)
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, mv.Z * spd)
        elseif M.arPhase == 2 then
            local tgt = Vector3.new(M.AP_R2.X, hrp.Position.Y, M.AP_R2.Z)
            if (tgt - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
                hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                M.autoRightEnabled = false
                if M.arConn then M.arConn:Disconnect(); M.arConn = nil end
                M.arPhase = 1
                if M.autoRightSetVisual then M.autoRightSetVisual(false) end
                if M.mobBtnRefs.autoRight then M.mobBtnRefs.autoRight(false) end
                return
            end
            local d = M.AP_R2 - hrp.Position
            local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z)
            if mv.Magnitude > 0.01 then mv = mv.Unit end
            hum:Move(mv, false)
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, mv.Z * spd)
        end
        if M.autoMoveSwingEnabled and not M._arSwingDebounce then
            M._arSwingDebounce = true
            local bat = M.findBat and M.findBat() or (M.findBatForAimbot and M.findBatForAimbot())
            if bat then
                if bat.Parent ~= char then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end) end
                pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
            end
            task.delay(M.autoMoveSwingInterval or 0.3, function() M._arSwingDebounce = false end)
        end
    end)
end

function M.enableAntiKick()
    M.antiKickEnabled = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while M.antiKickEnabled do
            task.wait(0.5)
            local char = player.Character
            if char then
                local found = false
                for _, tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    if tool:IsA("Tool") then
                        local n = tool.Name:lower()
                        if n:find("brainrot") or n:find("skibidi") or n:find("toilet") then
                            found = true
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
                M.brainrotDetected = found
                if found then
                    if M.autoBatEnabled then M.stopBatAimbot() end
                    if M.autoLeftEnabled then M.autoLeftEnabled=false; if M.autoLeftSetVisual then M.autoLeftSetVisual(false) end; M.stopAutoLeft() end
                    if M.autoRightEnabled then M.autoRightEnabled=false; if M.autoRightSetVisual then M.autoRightSetVisual(false) end; M.stopAutoRight() end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function M.disableAntiKick()
    M.antiKickEnabled = false
    M.brainrotDetected = false
end

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- SAFE MODE (locks combat during duel countdown / while holding brainrot)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
function M.safeModeGetCountdownLabel()
    local ok, label = pcall(function()
        local pg = player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if not pg then return nil end
        local top = pg:FindFirstChild("DuelsMachineTopFrame")
        if not top then return nil end
        local inner = top:FindFirstChild("DuelsMachineTopFrame")
        if not inner then return nil end
        local timer = inner:FindFirstChild("Timer")
        if not timer then return nil end
        return timer:FindFirstChild("Label")
    end)
    return (ok and label) or nil
end

function M.safeModeCountdownNumber(text)
    local t = tostring(text or ""):upper():gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
