-- Aplica o esquema de cor salvo ANTES de derivar a paleta
pcall(applyAccentFromTheme)

local UI_BLACK = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
local function uiMix(a, b, t) return a:Lerp(b, t) end

-- Defaults: pure black shell + white functions (image reference)
local UI_ACCENT       = UI_ACCENT or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
local UI_ACCENT_DIM   = UI_ACCENT_DIM or Color3.fromRGB(160, 165, 175)
local UI_ACCENT_SOFT  = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 235)
local UI_BG_DARK      = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
local UI_PANEL_BG     = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
local UI_ROW_BG       = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 20)
local UI_BTN_BG       = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 28)
local UI_CARD_STROKE  = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 46)
local UI_TEXT_WHITE   = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
local UI_TEXT_PRIMARY = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
local UI_TEXT_DIM     = Color3.fromRGB(170, 175, 185)
local UI_TEXT_SECTION = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
local UI_TOGGLE_OFF   = Color3.fromRGB(32, 32, 36)
local UI_TOGGLE_KNOB  = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 230)
local UI_KNOB_ON      = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
local UI_GRAD_TOP     = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 14)
local UI_GRAD_BOT     = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)

local UI_TWEEN_FAST = TweenInfo.new(0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
local UI_TWEEN_MED  = TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
local UI_TWEEN_SNAP = TweenInfo.new(0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)

-- Métricas — glass density
local UI_ROW_H       = 44
local UI_SECTION_H   = 28
local UI_CARD_RADIUS = 14
local UI_PAD_X       = 14
local UI_RAIL_W      = 104

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- PRIMITIVAS DE UI (nova identidade visual)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------
local function uiCorner(obj, r)
    local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
    c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r or UI_CARD_RADIUS)
    c.Parent = obj
    return c
end

local function uiStroke(obj, color, thickness, transparency)
    local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    s.Color = color or UI_CARD_STROKE
    s.Thickness = thickness or 1
    s.Transparency = transparency == nil and 0.4 or transparency
    s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    s.Parent = obj
    return s
end

local function uiCardStyle(f)
    -- Premium glass card: soft radius + dual-tone stroke
    uiCorner(f, UI_CARD_RADIUS)
    local st = f:FindFirstChild("CardStroke")
    if not st then
        st = uiStroke(f, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 1.15, 0.78)
        if st then st.Name = "CardStroke" end
    else
        st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        st.Thickness = 1.15
        st.Transparency = 0.78
    end
    if st then
        pcall(function() st.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border end)
    end
    -- subtle top sheen
    if not f:FindFirstChild("GlassSheen") then
        local sheen = Instance.new("Frame")
        sheen.Name = "GlassSheen"
        sheen.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        sheen.BorderSizePixel = 0
        sheen.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
        sheen.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        sheen.ZIndex = (f.ZIndex or 1) + 1
        sheen.Parent = f
        local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        g.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)),
        })
        g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.92),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.97),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
        })
        g.Rotation = 0
        g.Parent = sheen
    end
end

local function uiSmallBtn(p)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
    b.Position = p.Pos or UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    b.Size = p.Size or UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 24)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = p.Bg or UI_BTN_BG
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.Text = p.Text or ""
    b.TextColor3 = p.Col or UI_TEXT_PRIMARY
    b.TextSize = p.TS or 11
    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    b.AutoButtonColor = false
    b.ZIndex = p.Z or 1
    b.Parent = p.Parent
    uiCorner(b, p.CR or 8)
    uiStroke(b, UI_CARD_STROKE, 1, 0.55)
    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {BackgroundColor3 = uiMix(UI_BTN_BG, UI_ACCENT, 0.22)}):Play()
    end)
    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {BackgroundColor3 = p.Bg or UI_BTN_BG}):Play()
    end)
    return b
end

-- Marcador vertical de accent na borda esquerda da linha
local function uiAccentBar(parent, on)
    local b = Instance.new("Frame")
    b.Name = "AccentTick"
    b.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -11)
    b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 22)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = UI_ACCENT
    b.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.ZIndex = 3
    b.Parent = parent
    uiCorner(b, 2)
    return b
end

local function uiAutoCanvas(scroll)
    local lay = scroll:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout"); if not lay then return end
    local pad = scroll:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIPadding")
    local function upd()
        local padBottom = (pad and pad.PaddingBottom.Offset or 0)
        local padTop = (pad and pad.PaddingTop.Offset or 0)
        local h = lay.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + padBottom + padTop + 20
        scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(h, 1))
    end
    lay:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(upd)
    task.defer(upd)
    task.delay(0.15, upd)
    task.delay(0.5, upd)
end

-- Cabeçalho de seção: número/rótulo + régua de accent
local function uiSectionHeader(parent, text)
    local r = Instance.new("Frame")
    r.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI_SECTION_H)
    r.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    r.Parent = parent

    local badge = Instance.new("Frame")
    badge.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -9)
    badge.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 18)
    badge.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
    badge.BackgroundColor3 = uiMix(UI_ROW_BG, UI_ACCENT, 0.14)
    badge.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    badge.BorderSizePixel = 0
    badge.Parent = r
    uiCorner(badge, 9)
    local bst = uiStroke(badge, UI_ACCENT, 1, 0.72)

    local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
    dot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0.5, -3)
    dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6)
    dot.BackgroundColor3 = UI_ACCENT
    dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
    dot.Parent = badge
    uiCorner(dot, 3)

    local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 0)
    l.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    l.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
    l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    l.Text = "  " .. string.upper(tostring(text or "")) .. "  "
    l.TextColor3 = UI_ACCENT_SOFT or UI_TEXT_SECTION
    l.TextSize = 10
    l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    l.Parent = badge

    local line = Instance.new("Frame")
    line.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -2)
    line.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
    line.BackgroundColor3 = UI_ACCENT
    line.BackgroundTransparency = 0.88
    line.BorderSizePixel = 0
    line.Parent = r
    local lg = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    lg.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, 0.4),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
    })
    lg.Parent = line
    return r
end

local function uiRowBase(parent, height, hidden)
    -- Blody glass row: higher transparency + soft hover target
    local r = Instance.new("Frame")
    r.ClipsDescendants = true
    r.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, (height or UI_ROW_H) + 4)
    r.BackgroundColor3 = UI_ROW_BG
    r.BackgroundTransparency = 0.42
    r.BorderSizePixel = 0
    if hidden then r.Visible = false end
    r.Parent = parent
    uiCardStyle(r)
    r.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        pcall(function()
            TweenService:Create(r, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.16,
                BackgroundColor3 = uiMix(UI_ROW_BG, UI_ACCENT, 0.08)
            }):Play()
        end)
    end)
    r.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        pcall(function()
            TweenService:Create(r, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.42,
                BackgroundColor3 = UI_ROW_BG
            }):Play()
        end)
    end)
    return r
end

local function uiRowLabel(parent, text, rightGap)
    local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    l.Position = UDim2.new(0, UI_PAD_X, 0, 0)
    l.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(rightGap or 80), 1, 0)
    l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    l.Text = text
    l.TextColor3 = UI_TEXT_PRIMARY
    l.TextSize = 13
    l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    l.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    l.Parent = parent
    return l
end

local function uiStyleField(bx)
    bx.BackgroundColor3 = UI_BTN_BG
    bx.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bx.TextColor3 = UI_ACCENT_SOFT
    bx.TextSize = 12
    bx.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    uiCorner(bx, 8)
    uiStroke(bx, UI_ACCENT, 1, 0.72)
end

local function uiInputRow(parent, label, def, hidden)
    local r = uiRowBase(parent, UI_ROW_H, hidden)
    uiRowLabel(r, label, 88)
    local bx = Instance.new("TextBox")
    bx.Position = UDim2.new(1, -68, 0.5, -12)
    bx.Size = UDim2.new(0, 54, 0, 24)
    bx.Text = tostring(def)
    bx.Parent = r
    uiStyleField(bx)
    return r, bx
end

-- Interruptor: trilho fino com knob e brilho de accent
local function uiMakeSwitch(parent, on)
    local tb = Instance.new("TextButton")
    tb.Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0.5, -11)
    tb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 22)
    tb.BackgroundColor3 = on and UI_ACCENT or UI_TOGGLE_OFF
    tb.BorderSizePixel = 0
    tb.Text = ""
    tb.AutoButtonColor = false
    tb.Parent = parent
    uiCorner(tb, 11)
    local st = uiStroke(tb, UI_ACCENT, 1, on and 0.3 or 0.78)

    local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
    knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
    knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
    knob.Position = on and UDim2.new(1, -19, 0.5, -8) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -8)
    knob.BackgroundColor3 = on and UI_KNOB_ON or UI_TOGGLE_KNOB
    knob.Parent = tb
    uiCorner(knob, 8)

    local function paint(v)
        TweenService:Create(tb, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {BackgroundColor3 = v and UI_ACCENT or UI_TOGGLE_OFF}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(st, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {Transparency = v and 0.3 or 0.78}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(knob, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {
            Position = v and UDim2.new(1, -19, 0.5, -8) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -8),
            BackgroundColor3 = v and UI_KNOB_ON or UI_TOGGLE_KNOB
        }):Play()
    end
    return tb, paint
end

local function uiToggleRow(parent, label, on, callback)
    local r = uiRowBase(parent, UI_ROW_H)
    local bar = uiAccentBar(r, on)
    uiRowLabel(r, label, 74)
    local tb, paint = uiMakeSwitch(r, on)

    local state = on
    local function set(v)
        state = v
        paint(v)
        TweenService:Create(bar, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {BackgroundTransparency = v and 0 or 1}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(r, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {BackgroundColor3 = v and uiMix(UI_ROW_BG, UI_ACCENT, 0.10) or UI_ROW_BG}):Play()
    end
    if on then r.BackgroundColor3 = uiMix(UI_ROW_BG, UI_ACCENT, 0.10) end
    tb.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        set(not state)
        if callback then callback(state) end
        saveCherryConfig()
    end)
    return r, set
end

local function uiActionRow(parent, label, callback)
    local r = uiRowBase(parent, UI_ROW_H - 2)
    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    btn.Text = label
    btn.TextColor3 = UI_TEXT_PRIMARY
    btn.TextSize = 13
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    btn.Parent = r
    local bar = uiAccentBar(r, false)

    btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(r, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {BackgroundColor3 = uiMix(UI_ROW_BG, UI_ACCENT, 0.16)}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(btn, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {TextColor3 = UI_ACCENT_SOFT}):Play()
    end)
    btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(r, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {BackgroundColor3 = UI_ROW_BG}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(btn, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {TextColor3 = UI_TEXT_PRIMARY}):Play()
    end)
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(bar, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {BackgroundTransparency = 0}):Play()
        task.delay(0.3, function()
            TweenService:Create(bar, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
        end)
        if callback then callback() end
    end)
    return r, btn
end

local function uiNumberRow(parent, label, value, minV, maxV, callback)
    local r = uiRowBase(parent, UI_ROW_H)
    uiRowLabel(r, label, 168)

    local function clamp(n)
        n = tonumber(n)
        if not n then return nil end
        return math.clamp(n, minV or 0, maxV or 1e9)
    end

    local bx = Instance.new("TextBox")
    bx.Position = UDim2.new(1, -114, 0.5, -12)
    bx.Size = UDim2.new(0, 52, 0, 24)
    bx.Text = tostring(value)
    bx.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    bx.Parent = r
    uiStyleField(bx)

    local function apply(n)
        n = clamp(n)
        if not n then bx.Text = tostring(value); return end
        value = n
        bx.Text = tostring(n)
        if callback then callback(n) end
        saveCherryConfig()
    end
    bx.FocusLost:Connect(function() apply(bx.Text) end)

    local minus = uiSmallBtn({
        Parent = r, Pos = UDim2.new(1, -152, 0.5, -12), Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 24),
        Text = "−", Col = UI_TEXT_PRIMARY, TS = 15, CR = 8
    })
    local plus = uiSmallBtn({
        Parent = r, Pos = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0.5, -12), Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 24),
        Text = "+", Col = UI_TEXT_PRIMARY, TS = 15, CR = 8
    })
    local function step()
        return (maxV and maxV <= 10) and 0.1 or 1
    end
    minus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() apply((tonumber(value) or 0) - step()) end)
    plus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() apply((tonumber(value) or 0) + step()) end)

    return r, bx
end

-- uiChoiceRow defined after style helpers (expand + pattern)

-- Seta do painel expansível: quadrado discreto com contorno de accent
local function styleArrowButton(arrow)
    arrow.BackgroundColor3 = UI_BTN_BG
    arrow.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    arrow.TextColor3 = UI_ACCENT_SOFT
    arrow.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
    local s = arrow:FindFirstChild("ArrowStroke")
    if not s then
        s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        s.Name = "ArrowStroke"
        s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        s.Parent = arrow
    end
    s.Color = UI_ACCENT
    s.Thickness = 1
    s.Transparency = 0.55
end

-- Chip de opção: pílula preenchida com accent quando ativa
local function styleOptionChip(btn, active)
    -- High-contrast chips (Glass Dark was making Tap/Hold look "invisible")
    btn.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
    btn.TextTransparency = 0
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    if active then
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = UI_ACCENT
        btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    else
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = uiMix(UI_BTN_BG, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 0.08)
        btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 232, 240)
    end
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    local stroke = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
    if not stroke then
        stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        stroke.Parent = btn
    end
    stroke.Color = active and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or UI_ACCENT
    stroke.Thickness = active and 1.2 or 1
    stroke.Transparency = active and 0.25 or 0.55
end

local function uiChoiceRow(parent, label, options, defaultIndex, callback)
    -- Expandable selector: "Label    current  [+]" → options list below
    options = options or {}
    local idx = math.clamp(tonumber(defaultIndex) or 1, 1, math.max(1, #options))

    local container = Instance.new("Frame")
    container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    container.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI_ROW_H)
    container.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    container.ClipsDescendants = false
    container.Parent = parent

    local col = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    col.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical
    col.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    col.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    col.Parent = container

    local r = Instance.new("Frame")
    r.LayoutOrder = 1
    r.ClipsDescendants = true
    r.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI_ROW_H)
    r.BackgroundColor3 = UI_ROW_BG
    r.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
    r.BorderSizePixel = 0
    r.Parent = container
    uiCardStyle(r)

    uiRowLabel(r, label, 150)

    local curLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    curLbl.Name = "CurrentValue"
    curLbl.Position = UDim2.new(1, -120, 0.5, -10)
    curLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 78, 0, 20)
    curLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    curLbl.Text = tostring(options[idx] or "")
    curLbl.TextColor3 = UI_ACCENT
    curLbl.TextSize = 11
    curLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    curLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    curLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    curLbl.Parent = r

    local expanded = false
    local plusBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    plusBtn.Name = "ExpandPlus"
    plusBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0.5, -12)
    plusBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 24)
    plusBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    plusBtn.Text = "+"
    plusBtn.TextSize = 16
    plusBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    plusBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    plusBtn.Parent = r
    uiCorner(plusBtn, 8)
    styleArrowButton(plusBtn)
    plusBtn.Text = "+"

    local optFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
    optFrame.Name = "OptionsPanel"
    optFrame.LayoutOrder = 2
    optFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    optFrame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    optFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    optFrame.Visible = false
    optFrame.Parent = container

    local many = #options > 5
    local optParent = optFrame
    if many then
        local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        scroll.Name = "OptionsScroll"
        scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, math.min(180, #options * 34))
        scroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
        scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 4
        scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = UI_ACCENT
        scroll.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
        scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        scroll.Parent = optFrame
        local sPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        sPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4)
        sPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4)
        sPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 2)
        sPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4)
        sPad.Parent = scroll
        optParent = scroll
    end

    local optLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    optLay.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical
    optLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)
    optLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    optLay.Parent = optParent

    local optionBtns = {}

    local function refreshOptionVisuals()
        for i, b in ipairs(optionBtns) do
            styleOptionChip(b, i == idx)
        end
        curLbl.Text = tostring(options[idx] or "")
    end

    for i, name in ipairs(options) do
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
        b.Name = "Opt_" .. tostring(i)
        b.LayoutOrder = i
        b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.Text = tostring(name)
        b.TextSize = 12
        b.AutoButtonColor = false
        b.Parent = optParent
        uiCorner(b, 8)
        styleOptionChip(b, i == idx)
        optionBtns[i] = b
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            idx = i
            refreshOptionVisuals()
            if callback then callback(options[idx]) end
            saveCherryConfig()
        end)
    end

    local function setExpanded(v)
        expanded = v and true or false
        optFrame.Visible = expanded
        plusBtn.Text = expanded and "−" or "+"
    end

    plusBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        setExpanded(not expanded)
    end)

    local function setVal(v)
        if type(v) == "number" then
            idx = math.clamp(math.floor(v), 1, math.max(1, #options))
        else
            for i, o in ipairs(options) do
                if o == v then idx = i; break end
            end
        end
        refreshOptionVisuals()
    end

    return container, setVal
end

local function uiExpandToggleRow(parent, label, on, options, defaultIndex, onToggle, onOption)
    local container = Instance.new("Frame")
    container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    container.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI_ROW_H)
    container.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    container.ClipsDescendants = false
    container.Parent = parent

    local col = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    col.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical
    col.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    col.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    col.Parent = container

    -- LINHA PRINCIPAL
    local r = Instance.new("Frame")
    r.LayoutOrder = 1
    r.ClipsDescendants = true
    r.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI_ROW_H)
    r.BackgroundColor3 = on and uiMix(UI_ROW_BG, UI_ACCENT, 0.10) or UI_ROW_BG
    r.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
    r.BorderSizePixel = 0
    r.Parent = container
    uiCardStyle(r)

    local bar = uiAccentBar(r, on)
    uiRowLabel(r, label, 112)

    local expanded = false
    local arrow = Instance.new("TextButton")
    arrow.Name = "ArrowButton"
    arrow.Position = UDim2.new(1, -96, 0.5, -12)
    arrow.Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 24)
    arrow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    arrow.Text = "+"
    arrow.TextSize = 11
    arrow.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    arrow.AutoButtonColor = false
    arrow.Parent = r
    uiCorner(arrow, 8)
    styleArrowButton(arrow)

    local tb, paint = uiMakeSwitch(r, on)

    -- OPÇÕES
    local useScroll = #options > 4
    local optFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
    optFrame.LayoutOrder = 2
    optFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, useScroll and 140 or 40)
    optFrame.BackgroundColor3 = UI_PANEL_BG
    optFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
    optFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    optFrame.Visible = false
    optFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
    optFrame.Parent = container
    uiCardStyle(optFrame)

    local optPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    optPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
    optPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
    optPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6)
    optPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6)
    optPad.Parent = optFrame

    local optParent = optFrame
    if useScroll then
        local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        scroll.Name = "OptionsScroll"
        scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, -4)
        scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2)
        scroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
        scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 4
        scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = UI_ACCENT
        scroll.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
        scroll.ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Always
        scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        scroll.Parent = optFrame
        local sPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        sPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4)
        sPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
        sPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4)
        sPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8)
        sPad.Parent = scroll
        local sLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        sLay.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical
        sLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)
        sLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        sLay.Parent = scroll
        optParent = scroll
    else
        local optLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        optLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
        optLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
        optLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
        optLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        optLayout.Parent = optFrame
    end

    -- AJUSTES DO MODO SELECIONADO
    local settingsHost = Instance.new("Frame")
    settingsHost.Name = "ModeSettings"
    settingsHost.LayoutOrder = 3
    settingsHost.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    settingsHost.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    settingsHost.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    settingsHost.Visible = false
    settingsHost.Parent = container

    local settingsLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    settingsLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    settingsLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    settingsLayout.Parent = settingsHost

    local idx = defaultIndex or 1
    local optionBtns = {}
    local state = on
    local modeSettings = {}

    local function refreshOptionVisuals()
        for i, b in ipairs(optionBtns) do styleOptionChip(b, i == idx) end
    end

    local settingsOnly = (#options == 0)
    local function forcePageCanvas()
        task.defer(function()
            local page = container and container.Parent
            if not (page and page:IsA("ScrollingFrame")) then return end
            local lay = page:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
            if not lay then return end
            local pad = page:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIPadding")
            local padBottom = (pad and pad.PaddingBottom.Offset or 0)
            local padTop = (pad and pad.PaddingTop.Offset or 0)
            local h = lay.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + padBottom + padTop + 28
            page.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(h, 1))
        end)
    end
    local function refreshModeSettings()
        local any = false
        for lab, fr in pairs(modeSettings) do
            local show = expanded and (settingsOnly or (options[idx] and lab == options[idx]))
            fr.Visible = show
            if show then
                any = true
                pcall(function()
                    fr.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                    fr.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
                end)
            end
        end
        settingsHost.Visible = any
        if any then
            pcall(function()
                settingsHost.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                settingsHost.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
            end)
        end
        forcePageCanvas()
    end

    for i, opt in ipairs(options) do
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
        b.LayoutOrder = i
        if useScroll then
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 32)
        else
            -- wider chips so Tap/Hold never look like empty slivers
            b.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.max(96, #tostring(opt) * 12 + 36), 0, 32)
        end
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.Text = tostring(opt)
        b.TextSize = 13
        b.TextTransparency = 0
        b.ZIndex = 5
        b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        b.TextXAlignment = useScroll and Enum.TextXAlignment.Left or Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        b.AutoButtonColor = false
        b.Parent = optParent
        uiCorner(b, 8)
        if useScroll then
            local p = Instance.new("UIPadding")
            p.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10)
            p.Parent = b
        end
        styleOptionChip(b, i == idx)
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            idx = i
            refreshOptionVisuals()
            refreshModeSettings()
            if onOption then onOption(options[idx]) end
            saveCherryConfig()
        end)
        optionBtns[i] = b
    end

    local function setExpanded(v)
        expanded = v
        -- Always show option chips when expanded and options exist (Manual / Hold etc.)
        -- Hide only for settings-only rows (empty options list)
        if settingsOnly or #options == 0 then
            optFrame.Visible = false
        else
            optFrame.Visible = v
            if v then
                optFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, useScroll and 140 or 48)
                optFrame.BackgroundColor3 = UI_PANEL_BG
                optFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.02
                optFrame.ClipsDescendants = false
                -- force chip visibility after expand (theme race / zero alpha)
                for _, b in ipairs(optionBtns) do
                    if b then
                        b.Visible = true
                        b.TextTransparency = 0
                        b.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                    end
                end
                refreshOptionVisuals()
            end
        end
        arrow.Text = v and "−" or "+"
        refreshModeSettings()
        forcePageCanvas()
        task.defer(function()
            task.wait()
            forcePageCanvas()
        end)
    end
    arrow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setExpanded(not expanded) end)

    local function set(v)
        state = v
        paint(v)
        TweenService:Create(bar, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {BackgroundTransparency = v and 0 or 1}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(r, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {BackgroundColor3 = v and uiMix(UI_ROW_BG, UI_ACCENT, 0.10) or UI_ROW_BG}):Play()
    end

    tb.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        set(not state)
        if onToggle then onToggle(state) end
        saveCherryConfig()
    end)

    local function setOption(v)
        local target = tostring(v or "")
        for i, o in ipairs(options) do
            if o == v or tostring(o):lower() == target:lower() then
                idx = i
                refreshOptionVisuals()
                refreshModeSettings()
                break
            end
        end
    end

    local function registerModeSettings(optionLabel, frame)
        frame.Parent = settingsHost
        frame.Visible = false
        frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        frame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        frame.ClipsDescendants = false
        modeSettings[optionLabel] = frame
        -- ensure list layout exists
        if not frame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout") then
            local sLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
            sLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
            sLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
            sLay.Parent = frame
        end
        task.defer(refreshModeSettings)
    end

    return container, set, setOption, registerModeSettings, function() return options[idx] end
end

local function uiMakePage(parent, name, order, vis)
    local p = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    p.Name = name
    p.Visible = vis ~= false
    p.LayoutOrder = order
    p.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    p.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    p.BorderSizePixel = 0
    p.ScrollBarThickness = 3
    p.ScrollBarImageColor3 = UI_ACCENT
    p.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.25
    p.ScrollingEnabled = true
    p.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
    p.ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Always
    p.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    p.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    p.Parent = parent
    local l = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    l.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
    l.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    l.Parent = p
    local pd = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    pd.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6)
    pd.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 36)
    pd.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6)
    pd.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4)
    pd.Parent = p
    uiAutoCanvas(p)
    return p
end

-- ABAS HORIZONTAIS NO TOPO
local function uiStyleTab(b, active)
    b:SetAttribute("IsActiveTab", active and true or false)
    local ind = b:FindFirstChild("TabIndicator")
    local lbl = b:FindFirstChild("TabLabel")
    TweenService:Create(b, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {
        BackgroundColor3 = active and uiMix(UI_ACCENT, Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255), 0.12) or UI_PANEL_BG,
        BackgroundTransparency = active and 0.08 or 0.55
    }):Play()
    if ind then
        TweenService:Create(ind, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {
            BackgroundTransparency = active and 0 or 1,
            BackgroundColor3 = active and UI_ACCENT or UI_TEXT_DIM,
            Size = active and UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 3) or UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 3)
        }):Play()
    end
    if lbl then
        TweenService:Create(lbl, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {
            TextColor3 = active and UI_TEXT_WHITE or UI_TEXT_DIM,
            TextTransparency = active and 0 or 0.25
        }):Play()
    end
    local st = b:FindFirstChild("TabStroke")
    if st then
        st.Color = active and UI_ACCENT or Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        st.Transparency = active and 0.25 or 0.82
        st.Thickness = active and 1.2 or 1
    end
end

local function uiMakeTab(parent, name, text, pos, active)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
    b.Name = name
    b.ZIndex = 9
    if pos then b.Position = pos end
    -- horizontal tab: fixed width, full height of tab bar (fits frame width 380)
    b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 64, 1, 0)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = UI_PANEL_BG
    b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.Text = ""
    b.AutoButtonColor = false
    b.Parent = parent
    uiCorner(b, 8)
    local stroke = uiStroke(b, UI_ACCENT, 1, 0.85)
    stroke.Name = "TabStroke"

    -- indicator as bottom underline (horizontal tabs)
    local ind = Instance.new("Frame")
    ind.Name = "TabIndicator"
    ind.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
    ind.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -2)
    ind.Size = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 3)
    ind.BackgroundColor3 = UI_ACCENT
    ind.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ind.BorderSizePixel = 0
    ind.ZIndex = 10
    ind.Parent = b
    uiCorner(ind, 2)

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.Name = "TabLabel"
    lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = text
    lbl.TextColor3 = UI_TEXT_DIM
    lbl.TextSize = 10
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    lbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    lbl.ZIndex = 10
    lbl.Parent = b

    uiStyleTab(b, active)

    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if not b:GetAttribute("IsActiveTab") then
            TweenService:Create(b, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.15}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(lbl, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {TextColor3 = UI_TEXT_PRIMARY}):Play()
        end
    end)
    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if not b:GetAttribute("IsActiveTab") then uiStyleTab(b, false) end
    end)
    return b
end

-- MAIN BUILD
function M.applyCustomBackground(frame)
    if not frame then return end
    local existing = frame:FindFirstChild("CustomBgImage")
    if existing then existing:Destroy() end
    local id = tonumber(M.customBgId) or 0
    if id <= 0 then return end
    local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    img.Name = "CustomBgImage"
    img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    img.Image = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)
    img.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
    img.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    img.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0)
    img.ZIndex = 0
    img.ImageTransparency = math.clamp(tonumber(M.customBgOpacity) or 0.35, 0, 1)
    img.Parent = frame
end

function M.openImagePicker(kind)
    -- kind = "bg" | "mob"
    local isBg = kind == "bg"
    local ids = isBg and M.BG_IMAGE_IDS or M.MOB_BTN_IMAGE_IDS
    local title = isBg and "CUSTOM BG" or "BUTTON BG"
    local currentId = isBg and (tonumber(M.customBgId) or 0) or (tonumber(M.mobBtnBgId) or 0)
    local opacity = math.clamp(tonumber(M.customBgOpacity) or 0.35, 0, 1)

    local old = player.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ZinthImagePicker")
    if old then old:Destroy() end
    local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui"):FindFirstChild("ZinthImagePicker")
    if cg then cg:Destroy() end

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "ZinthImagePicker"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    gui.DisplayOrder = 120
    pcall(function() gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    if not gui.Parent then gui.Parent = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end

    -- dim backdrop (tap to close)
    local dim = Instance.new("TextButton")
    dim.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    dim.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    dim.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
    dim.Text = ""
    dim.AutoButtonColor = false
    dim.ZIndex = 1
    dim.Parent = gui
    dim.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() gui:Destroy() end)

    local panel = Instance.new("Frame")
    panel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    panel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    panel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, isBg and 280 or 230)
    panel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 16)
    panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
    panel.ZIndex = 2
    panel.ClipsDescendants = true
    panel.Parent = gui
    Instance.new("UICorner", panel).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)
    local pst = Instance.new("UIStroke", panel)
    pst.Color = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 60)
    pst.Thickness = 1

    local hdr = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    hdr.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 28)
    hdr.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 6)
    hdr.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    hdr.Text = title
    hdr.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 235)
    hdr.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    hdr.TextSize = 12
    hdr.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    hdr.ZIndex = 3
    hdr.Parent = panel

    local close = Instance.new("TextButton")
    close.Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 24)
    close.Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0, 6)
    close.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    close.Text = "×"
    close.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 190)
    close.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    close.TextSize = 18
    close.ZIndex = 3
    close.Parent = panel
    close.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() gui:Destroy() end)

    local preview = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    preview.Name = "Preview"
    preview.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 100)
    preview.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 34)
    preview.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 24)
    preview.BorderSizePixel = 0
    preview.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
    preview.Image = currentId > 0 and ("rbxassetid://" .. currentId) or ""
    preview.ImageTransparency = isBg and opacity or 0
    preview.ZIndex = 3
    preview.Parent = panel
    Instance.new("UICorner", preview).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)

    local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 56)
    scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 142)
    scroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
    scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 4
    scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = UI_ACCENT or Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
    scroll.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.X
    scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
    scroll.ZIndex = 3
    scroll.Parent = panel

    local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    lay.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
    lay.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
    lay.Parent = scroll

    local selectedId = currentId

    local function selectId(id)
        selectedId = id
        preview.Image = id > 0 and ("rbxassetid://" .. id) or ""
        if isBg then
            M.customBgId = id
            if M.mainFrame then M.applyCustomBackground(M.mainFrame) end
        else
            M.mobBtnBgId = id
            if M.mobileButtonsEnabled then M.buildMobileButtons() end
        end
        saveCherryConfig()
    end

    -- None option
    local none = Instance.new("TextButton")
    none.Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 48)
    none.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 34)
    none.Text = "OFF"
    none.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210)
    none.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    none.TextSize = 10
    none.ZIndex = 4
    none.Parent = scroll
    Instance.new("UICorner", none).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    none.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() selectId(0) end)

    for _, id in ipairs(ids) do
        local thumb = Instance.new("ImageButton")
        thumb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 48)
        thumb.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 34)
        thumb.Image = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)
        thumb.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        thumb.ZIndex = 4
        thumb.Parent = scroll
        Instance.new("UICorner", thumb).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", thumb)
        st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        st.Transparency = (id == currentId) and 0.2 or 0.7
        st.Thickness = 1
        thumb.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            selectId(id)
            for _, ch in ipairs(scroll:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("ImageButton") then
                    local s = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                    if s then s.Transparency = 0.7 end
                end
            end
            st.Transparency = 0.2
        end)
    end

    if isBg then
        local opLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        opLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -12, 0, 18)
        opLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 208)
        opLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        opLbl.Text = "OPACITY"
        opLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 170)
        opLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        opLbl.TextSize = 10
        opLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        opLbl.ZIndex = 3
        opLbl.Parent = panel

        local opVal = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        opVal.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -12, 0, 18)
        opVal.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 208)
        opVal.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        opVal.Text = tostring(math.floor((1 - opacity) * 100)) .. "%"
        opVal.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210)
        opVal.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        opVal.TextSize = 10
        opVal.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        opVal.ZIndex = 3
        opVal.Parent = panel

        -- simple slider track
        local track = Instance.new("Frame")
        track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 8)
        track.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 232)
        track.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 48)
        track.BorderSizePixel = 0
        track.ZIndex = 3
        track.Parent = panel
        Instance.new("UICorner", track).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(1 - opacity, 0, 1, 0)
        fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 230)
        fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        fill.ZIndex = 4
        fill.Parent = track
        Instance.new("UICorner", fill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
        knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
        knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        knob.Position = UDim2.new(1 - opacity, 0, 0.5, 0)
        knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
        knob.ZIndex = 5
        knob.Parent = track
        Instance.new("UICorner", knob).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local dragging = false
        local function setFromX(x)
            local rel = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1)
            -- rel 1 = fully opaque image (low ImageTransparency)
            local imageTransparency = 1 - rel
            opacity = imageTransparency
            M.customBgOpacity = opacity
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(rel, 0, 1, 0)
            knob.Position = UDim2.new(rel, 0, 0.5, 0)
            preview.ImageTransparency = opacity
            opVal.Text = tostring(math.floor(rel * 100)) .. "%"
            if M.mainFrame then M.applyCustomBackground(M.mainFrame) end
            saveCherryConfig()
        end
        track.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                setFromX(i.Position.X)
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                setFromX(i.Position.X)
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = false
            end
        end)
    end
end


-- ============================================================
-- CUSTOM FONTS (from EXE)
-- ============================================================
function M._fontShouldTouch(obj)
    if not (obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox")) then return false end
    if obj.TextStrokeTransparency ~= 1 then return false end
    return true
end

function M._fontApplyOne(txt)
    if not M._fontShouldTouch(txt) then return end
    if not M._fontOrig[txt] then M._fontOrig[txt] = txt.FontFace end
    if M._fontMy then
        pcall(function() txt.FontFace = M._fontMy end)
    end
end

function M._fontSetupCoding()
    if M._fontMy and M.customFontSelected == "Coding Font" then return true end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        if isfile and writefile and getcustomasset then
            if not isfile("zinth_starborn.ttf") then
                writefile("zinth_starborn.ttf", game:HttpGet("https://granny.anondrop.net/uploads/6c2505542959f371/Starborn.ttf"))
            end
            writefile("zinth_starborn.json", HS:JSONEncode({
                name = "Starborn",
                faces = {{name = "Regular", weight = 400, style = "normal", assetId = getcustomasset("zinth_starborn.ttf")}}
            }))
            M._fontMy = Font.new(getcustomasset("zinth_starborn.json"))
        end
    end)
    return ok and M._fontMy ~= nil
end

function M.getFontForName(name)
    if not name or name == "None" then return nil end
    if name == "Coding Font" then
        if M._fontSetupCoding() then return M._fontMy end
        return nil
    elseif name == "Summer" then
        return Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/PermanentMarker.json")
    elseif name == "Beachy" then
        return Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/DenkOne.json")
    elseif name == "Scary" then
        return Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/Creepster.json")
    elseif name == "Bangers" then
        return Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/Bangers.json")
    end
    return nil
end

function M.applyCustomFont(name)
    if M._fontConn then pcall(function() M._fontConn:Disconnect() end); M._fontConn = nil end
    for obj, orig in pairs(M._fontOrig) do
        pcall(function() if obj and obj.Parent then obj.FontFace = orig end end)
    end
    M._fontOrig = {}
    M.customFontSelected = name or "None"
    if M._fontConn then
        pcall(function() M._fontConn:Disconnect() end)
        M._fontConn = nil
    end
    if name and name ~= "None" then
        local font = M.getFontForName(name)
        if font then
            M._fontMy = font
            -- Scope to PlayerGui only (game:GetDescendants is catastrophic for FPS)
            local pg = player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            if pg then
                for _, v in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
                    M._fontApplyOne(v)
                end
                M._fontConn = pg.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
                    if M.customFontSelected ~= "None" then M._fontApplyOne(obj) end
                end)
            end
            -- also recolor main hub frames if present
            if M.mainFrame then
                for _, v in ipairs(M.mainFrame:GetDescendants()) do
                    M._fontApplyOne(v)
                end
            end
        end
    else
        M._fontMy = nil
    end
    -- always persist selection
    pcall(function()
        if type(saveCherryConfig) == "function" then saveCherryConfig() end
    end)
end

function M.buildGui()
    applyAccentFromTheme()
    M.clearPersistentConns()

    for _,n in ipairs({"MoveeDuels","Cherry_Menu","K7HubGUI","VantaHubUI","ZinthHubUI","AxeHubUI","AceDuelsAdaptReconstruct"}) do
        local cg=game:GetService("CoreGui")
        local old=cg:FindFirstChild(n); if old then old:Destroy() end
        local pg=player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if pg then local o=pg:FindFirstChild(n); if o then o:Destroy() end end
    end

    M.buildStatusUI()

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "AxeHubUI"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    gui.Parent = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

    -- ========================================================
    -- JANELA PRINCIPAL — painel de vidro + rail lateral
    -- ========================================================
    local Frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    Frame.Name = "Frame"
    Frame.ClipsDescendants = true
    Frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0.5, -290)
    Frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 392, 0, 580)
    Frame.BackgroundColor3 = UI_BG_DARK
    Frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Frame.Active = true
    Frame.Parent = gui
    M.mainFrame = Frame
    M.applyCustomBackground(Frame)
    -- entrance scale pop
    do
        local openScale = Frame:FindFirstChild("BDUIScale")
        if openScale then
            local target = openScale.Scale
            openScale.Scale = target * 0.92
            TweenService:Create(openScale, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Scale = target}):Play()
        end
        Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
        TweenService:Create(Frame, TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {BackgroundTransparency = 0}):Play()
    end

    local UIScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
    UIScale.Name = "BDUIScale"
    UIScale.Scale = M.uiScale or 0.7
    UIScale.Parent = Frame
    M.uiScaleRef = UIScale

    local frameCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")

        stroke.Parent = Frame

        halo.Name = "AccentHalo"
        halo.Color = UI_ACCENT
        halo.Thickness = 2.5
        halo.Transparency = 0.88
        halo.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        halo.Parent = Frame
    end

    -- HEADER (glass strip)
    local Header = Instance.new("Frame")
    Header.Name = "Header"
    Header.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 58)

    Header.BackgroundColor3 = uiMix(UI_BG_DARK, UI_ACCENT, 0.08)
    Header.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
    Header.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Header.Active = true
    Header.Parent = Frame
    uiCorner(Header, 16)
    do

        if hs then hs.Name = "HeaderStroke" end

            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.55),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.85),
        })
        hg.Parent = Header
    end

    do
        -- selo quadrado com a inicial
        local mark = Instance.new("Frame")
        mark.ZIndex = 3

        mark.Size = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 34)
        mark.BackgroundColor3 = uiMix(UI_BG_DARK, UI_ACCENT, 0.22)
        mark.BorderSizePixel = 0

        uiStroke(mark, UI_ACCENT, 1.2, 0.35)

        local mi = Instance.new("ImageLabel")

        mi.ZIndex = 4
        mi.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        mi.BackgroundTransparency = 1

        mi.Parent = mark
        uiCorner(mi, 10)

        task.spawn(function()

                return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(

                    Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot,

                )

                mi.Image = content
            end

        local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        t.ZIndex = 3
        t.Position = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 10)
        t.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 22)
        t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        t.Text = "FLOW HUB"
        t.TextColor3 = UI_TEXT_WHITE
        t.TextSize = 18
        t.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        t.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        t.Parent = Header

        local s = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        s.ZIndex = 3
        s.Position = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 32)
        s.Size = UDim2.new(0, 240, 0, 14)
        s.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        s.Text = "FLOW ENGINE  ·  GLASS UI  ·  DUELS"
        s.TextColor3 = UI_TEXT_DIM
        s.TextSize = 10
        s.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        s.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        s.Parent = Header
    end

    local MinBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    MinBtn.ZIndex = 3
    MinBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -38, 0.5, -13)
    MinBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
    MinBtn.BackgroundColor3 = UI_BTN_BG
    MinBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    MinBtn.Text = "—"
    MinBtn.TextColor3 = UI_TEXT_PRIMARY
    MinBtn.TextSize = 13
    MinBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    MinBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    MinBtn.Parent = Header
    uiCorner(MinBtn, 8)
    uiStroke(MinBtn, UI_ACCENT, 1, 0.6)
    MinBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(MinBtn, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {BackgroundColor3 = uiMix(UI_BTN_BG, UI_ACCENT, 0.28)}):Play()
    end)
    MinBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(MinBtn, UI_TWEEN_FAST, {BackgroundColor3 = UI_BTN_BG}):Play()
    end)

    local lockButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
    lockButton.ZIndex = 3
    lockButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 62, 0, 24)
    lockButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 19)
    lockButton.BackgroundColor3 = UI_BTN_BG
    lockButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
    lockButton.Text = "UNLOCK"
    lockButton.TextColor3 = UI_TEXT_DIM
    lockButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    lockButton.TextSize = 9
    lockButton.AutoButtonColor = false
    lockButton.Parent = Header
    uiCorner(lockButton, 8)
    local lockStroke = uiStroke(lockButton, UI_ACCENT, 1, 0.7)

    -- Hub lock always forced OFF (user request): always unlocked + drag always works
    M.uiLocked = false
    local function paintLock()
        lockButton.Text = "UNLOCK"
        lockButton.TextColor3 = UI_TEXT_DIM
        lockButton.BackgroundColor3 = UI_BTN_BG
        lockStroke.Transparency = 0.7
    end
    paintLock()
    lockButton.Activated:Connect(function()
        -- do nothing: lock permanently disabled
        M.uiLocked = false
        paintLock()
    end)

    local Div = Instance.new("Frame")
    Div.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 62)
    Div.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
    Div.BackgroundColor3 = UI_ACCENT
    Div.BackgroundTransparency = 0.7
    Div.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Div.Parent = Frame

    -- ABAS HORIZONTAIS NO TOPO (logo abaixo do header)
    local TAB_BAR_H = 36
    local TAB_BAR_Y = 72 -- below header (8+58+6)
    local TabBar = Instance.new("Frame")
    TabBar.Name = "TabBar"
    TabBar.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    TabBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, TAB_BAR_Y)
    TabBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, TAB_BAR_H)
    TabBar.BackgroundColor3 = uiMix(UI_BG_DARK, UI_ACCENT, 0.06)
    TabBar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.22
    TabBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    TabBar.ZIndex = 5
    TabBar.Parent = Frame
    uiCorner(TabBar, 14)
    do
        local tbs = uiStroke(TabBar, Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255), 1.15, 0.72)
        if tbs then tbs.Name = "TabBarStroke" end
    end

    local TabsLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    TabsLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    TabsLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
    TabsLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
    TabsLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
    TabsLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    TabsLayout.Parent = TabBar

    local TabsPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    TabsPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4)
    TabsPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4)
    TabsPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 3)
    TabsPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 3)
    TabsPad.Parent = TabBar

    local TSpeed = uiMakeTab(TabBar,"Tab_SPEED","SPEED",nil,true); TSpeed.LayoutOrder=1
    local TMech  = uiMakeTab(TabBar,"Tab_MECH","MECH",nil,false); TMech.LayoutOrder=2
    local TVis   = uiMakeTab(TabBar,"Tab_VIS","VISUAL",nil,false); TVis.LayoutOrder=3
    local TUtil  = uiMakeTab(TabBar,"Tab_UTIL","UTIL",nil,false); TUtil.LayoutOrder=4
    local TKB    = uiMakeTab(TabBar,"Tab_KB","BINDS",nil,false); TKB.LayoutOrder=5; TKB.Visible = false -- keybinds moved to SPEED

    -- CONTEÚDO: abaixo do header + abas do topo
    local contentTop = TAB_BAR_Y + TAB_BAR_H + 8
    local contentBottomPad = 12
    local PagedContent = Instance.new("Frame")
    PagedContent.Name = "PagedContent"
    PagedContent.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, contentTop)
    PagedContent.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -(contentTop + contentBottomPad))
    PagedContent.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    PagedContent.Parent = Frame

    local PM    = uiMakePage(PagedContent, "Page_SPEED",     1, true)
    local PMech = uiMakePage(PagedContent, "Page_MECHANICS", 2, false)
    local PVis  = uiMakePage(PagedContent, "Page_VISUALS",   3, false)
    local PUtil = uiMakePage(PagedContent, "Page_UTILITY",   4, false)
    local PKB   = uiMakePage(PagedContent, "Page_KEYBINDS",  5, false)

    local Pages = {SPEED=PM, MECHANICS=PMech, VISUALS=PVis, UTILITY=PUtil, KEYBINDS=PKB}
    local Tabs  = {SPEED=TSpeed, MECHANICS=TMech, VISUALS=TVis, UTILITY=TUtil, KEYBINDS=TKB}
    local curTab = "SPEED"

    local function switchTab(name)
        if curTab == name then return end
        curTab = name
        for k, p in pairs(Pages) do
            p.Visible = (k == name)
            if p.Visible then
                p.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
                TweenService:Create(p, UI_TWEEN_MED, {Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)}):Play()
            end
        end
        for k, b in pairs(Tabs) do uiStyleTab(b, k == name) end
    end
    M.selectTab = switchTab

    TSpeed.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() switchTab("SPEED") end)
    TMech.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() switchTab("MECHANICS") end)
    TVis.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() switchTab("VISUALS") end)
    TUtil.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() switchTab("UTILITY") end)
    TKB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() switchTab("KEYBINDS") end)

    -- close / minimize
    local function closeUI()
        local tween = TweenService:Create(Frame, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 380, 0, 0),
            Position = Frame.Position + UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 278),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1
        })
        tween:Play()
        tween.Completed:Connect(function()
            Frame.Visible = false
            Frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 380, 0, 556)
            Frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0.5, -278)
            Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        end)
    end
    local MinPill = Instance.new("Frame")
    MinPill.Visible=false; MinPill.Active=true; MinPill.ZIndex=40
    MinPill.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    MinPill.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 10)
    MinPill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 156, 0, 34)
    MinPill.BackgroundColor3 = UI_BG_DARK
    MinPill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
    MinPill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    MinPill.Parent = gui
    uiCorner(MinPill, 10)
    do
        local pg = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        pg.Rotation = 90
        pg.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, uiMix(UI_BG_DARK, UI_ACCENT, 0.16)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, UI_BG_DARK),
        })
        pg.Parent = MinPill
        local pst = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        pst.Color = UI_ACCENT
        pst.Thickness = 1.2
        pst.Transparency = 0.35
        pst.Parent = MinPill
    end
    do
        local l=Instance.new("TextLabel"); l.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0); l.BackgroundTransparency=1; l.Text="flow.vs"; l.TextColor3=UI_ACCENT; l.TextSize=13; l.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack; l.Parent=MinPill
        local b=Instance.new("TextButton"); b.ZIndex=41; b.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0); b.BackgroundTransparency=1; b.Text=""; b.AutoButtonColor=false; b.Parent=MinPill
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            MinPill.Visible=false; Frame.Visible=true
            M.menuOpen = true
            pcall(saveCherryConfig)
        end)
    end

    local function minimize()
        Frame.Visible=false; MinPill.Visible=true

        M.menuOpen=false; pcall(saveCherryConfig)
    end
    MinBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(minimize)

    -- DRAGGING: UIS global end/move so release outside header always stops follow
    do
        local function makeDrag(obj, target)
            local drag, dStart, sPos = false, nil, nil
            obj.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
                if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                    or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    drag = true
                    dStart = i.Position
                    sPos = target.Position
                end
            end)
            -- Global move: keeps tracking even when cursor leaves the header
            local moveConn = UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
                if not drag or not dStart or not sPos then return end
                if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                    or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    local d = i.Position - dStart
                    target.Position = UDim2.new(
                        sPos.X.Scale, sPos.X.Offset + d.X,
                        sPos.Y.Scale, sPos.Y.Offset + d.Y
                    )
                end
            end)
            -- Global end: click-release ALWAYS clears drag (fixes sticky follow)
            local endConn = UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
                if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                    or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    drag = false
                    dStart, sPos = nil, nil
                end
            end)
            -- also clear if this specific input ends
            obj.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
                if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                    or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    drag = false
                    dStart, sPos = nil, nil
                end
            end)
        end
        makeDrag(Header, Frame)
        makeDrag(MinPill, MinPill)
    end

    -- KEYBIND CAPTURE
    M._anyKeyListening = false
    local activeKBBtn = nil
    M.keybindButtons = M.keybindButtons or {}
    local listeningTimeout = nil

    local function resetKeybindCapture()
        if activeKBBtn then
            for e,b in pairs(M.keybindButtons) do
                if b == activeKBBtn then
                    local parts = {}
                    if e.kb then table.insert(parts, e.kb.Name) end
                    if e.gp then table.insert(parts, e.gp.Name) end
                    b.Text = (#parts > 0) and table.concat(parts, " / ") or "..."
                    b.TextColor3 = UI_TEXT_DIM
                    break
                end
            end
            activeKBBtn = nil
            M._anyKeyListening = false
            if listeningTimeout then task.cancel(listeningTimeout); listeningTimeout = nil end
        end
    end

    local function formatKeybindText(entry)
        if not entry then return "..." end
        local parts = {}
        if entry.kb then table.insert(parts, entry.kb.Name) end
        if entry.gp then table.insert(parts, entry.gp.Name) end
        if #parts == 0 then return "..." end
        return table.concat(parts, " / ")
    end

    local function isGamepadInputType(uit)
        return uit == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1
            or uit == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad2
            or uit == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad3
            or uit == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad4
            or uit == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad5
            or uit == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad6
            or uit == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad7
            or uit == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad8
    end

    local function uiKeybindRow(parent, label, kbEntry)
        local r = Instance.new("Frame")
        r.ClipsDescendants = true
        r.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI_ROW_H)
        r.BackgroundColor3 = UI_ROW_BG
        r.BackgroundTransparency = 0.06
        r.BorderSizePixel = 0
        r.Parent = parent
        uiCardStyle(r)

        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        l.Position = UDim2.new(0, UI_PAD_X, 0, 0)
        l.Size = UDim2.new(0.42, 0, 1, 0)
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        l.Text = label
        l.TextColor3 = UI_TEXT_PRIMARY
        l.TextSize = 13
        l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        l.Parent = r

        local btn = uiSmallBtn({
            Parent = r,
            Pos = UDim2.new(1, -148, 0.5, -12),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 136, 0, 24),
            Text = formatKeybindText(kbEntry),
            Col = UI_TEXT_DIM,
            TS = 10,
            CR = 7
        })
        M.keybindButtons[kbEntry] = btn

        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if activeKBBtn and activeKBBtn ~= btn then resetKeybindCapture() end
            activeKBBtn = btn
            btn.Text = "Press key / button..."
            btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
            M._anyKeyListening = true
            if listeningTimeout then task.cancel(listeningTimeout) end
            listeningTimeout = task.delay(8, resetKeybindCapture)
        end)
        return r
    end

    local function kbMatch(entry, keycode)
        if not entry or not keycode or keycode == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then return false end
        if entry.kb and entry.kb == keycode then return true end
        if entry.gp and entry.gp == keycode then return true end
        return false
    end

    M._keybindCaptureConn = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
        -- Always capture while rebinding (even if gameProcessed)
        if M._anyKeyListening then
            if activeKBBtn then
                local kc = input.KeyCode
                if kc == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
                    resetKeybindCapture(); pcall(saveCherryConfig); return
                end
                local uit = input.UserInputType
                if uit == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and kc ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
                    for e,b in pairs(M.keybindButtons) do
                        if b == activeKBBtn then
                            e.kb = kc
                            -- keep existing gamepad bind so both PC + controller work
                            b.Text = formatKeybindText(e)
                            b.TextColor3 = UI_TEXT_DIM
                            activeKBBtn = nil; M._anyKeyListening = false
                            if listeningTimeout then task.cancel(listeningTimeout); listeningTimeout = nil end
                            pcall(saveCherryConfig)
                            break
                        end
                    end
                elseif isGamepadInputType(uit) and kc ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
                    for e,b in pairs(M.keybindButtons) do
                        if b == activeKBBtn then
                            e.gp = kc
                            -- keep existing keyboard bind
                            b.Text = formatKeybindText(e)
                            b.TextColor3 = UI_TEXT_DIM
                            activeKBBtn = nil; M._anyKeyListening = false
                            if listeningTimeout then task.cancel(listeningTimeout); listeningTimeout = nil end
                            pcall(saveCherryConfig)
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return
        end

        if gameProcessed then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and not isGamepadInputType(input.UserInputType) then return end
        local kc = input.KeyCode
        if kc == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then return end

        if kbMatch(M.KB.LaggerToggle, kc) then
            local now = tick()
            if not M._lastLaggerBindPress or now - M._lastLaggerBindPress > 0.15 then
                M._lastLaggerBindPress = now
                M.cycleLaggerModeBind()
            end
            return
        end
        if kbMatch(M.KB.SpeedToggle, kc) then M.toggleCarryMode(); saveCherryConfig() end
        if kbMatch(M.KB.DropBrainrot, kc) then M.runDrop() end
        if kbMatch(M.KB.TPFloor, kc) then M.runTPFloor() end
        if kbMatch(M.KB.AutoBat, kc) then
            if not M.autoBatEnabled then
                if M.autoLeftEnabled then M.autoLeftEnabled = false; M.stopAutoLeft() end
                if M.autoRightEnabled then M.autoRightEnabled = false; M.stopAutoRight() end
                M.queueAutoBatStart()
            else M.stopBatAimbot() end
            if M.autoBatSetVisual then M.autoBatSetVisual(M.autoBatEnabled) end
            if M.mobBtnRefs.autoBat then M.mobBtnRefs.autoBat(M.autoBatEnabled) end
            saveCherryConfig()
        end
        if kbMatch(M.KB.BypassAimbot, kc) then
            M.toggleBypassAimbot()
            if M.setBypassVisual then M.setBypassVisual(M.bypassAimbotEnabled) end
            if M.mobBtnRefs.bypass then M.mobBtnRefs.bypass(M.bypassAimbotEnabled) end
            saveCherryConfig()
        end
        if kbMatch(M.KB.AutoLeft, kc) then
            M.autoLeftEnabled = not M.autoLeftEnabled
            if M.autoLeftEnabled then
                if M.autoRightEnabled then M.autoRightEnabled = false; M.stopAutoRight() end
                if M.autoBatEnabled then M.stopBatAimbot() end
                M.startAutoLeft()
            else M.stopAutoLeft() end
            if M.autoLeftSetVisual then M.autoLeftSetVisual(M.autoLeftEnabled) end
            if M.mobBtnRefs.autoLeft then M.mobBtnRefs.autoLeft(M.autoLeftEnabled) end
            saveCherryConfig()
        end
        if kbMatch(M.KB.AutoRight, kc) then
            M.autoRightEnabled = not M.autoRightEnabled
            if M.autoRightEnabled then
                if M.autoLeftEnabled then M.autoLeftEnabled = false; M.stopAutoLeft() end
                if M.autoBatEnabled then M.stopBatAimbot() end
                M.startAutoRight()
            else M.stopAutoRight() end
            if M.autoRightSetVisual then M.autoRightSetVisual(M.autoRightEnabled) end
            if M.mobBtnRefs.autoRight then M.mobBtnRefs.autoRight(M.autoRightEnabled) end
            saveCherryConfig()
        end
        if kbMatch(M.KB.AntiBatTP, kc) then
            M.toggleAntiBatTP()
            if M.setAntiBatTPVisual then M.setAntiBatTPVisual(M.antiBatTPEnabled) end
            if M.mobBtnRefs.antiBatTP then M.mobBtnRefs.antiBatTP(M.antiBatTPEnabled) end
            saveCherryConfig()
        end
        if kbMatch(M.KB.GuiHide, kc) then
            if Frame then
                Frame.Visible = not Frame.Visible
                MinPill.Visible = not Frame.Visible
                M.menuOpen = Frame.Visible == true
                pcall(saveCherryConfig)
            end
        end
    end)

    -- ============================================================
    -- PAGINE
    -- ============================================================

    -- PAGE: SPEED
    uiSectionHeader(PM, "SPEEDS")
    local _, nsBox = uiNumberRow(PM, "Normal Speed", M.NS, 1, 500, function(v) M.NS = v end)

    local _, lcBox = uiNumberRow(PM, "Lagger Carry", math.min(M.LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED,23), 1, 23, function(v) M.LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED = math.min(v,23) end)

    uiKeybindRow(PM, "Lagger Mode",    M.KB.LaggerToggle)
    uiKeybindRow(PM, "Bat Aimbot",     M.KB.AutoBat)

    uiKeybindRow(PM, "Auto Right",     M.KB.AutoRight)
    uiKeybindRow(PM, "Drop Brainrot",  M.KB.DropBrainrot)

    uiKeybindRow(PM, "TP Bat",         M.KB.BypassAimbot)

        end
    )
    M.setAntiRagModeUI = setAntiRagModeUI

    local _, setMedusa = uiToggleRow(PMech, "Medusa Counter", M.medusaCounterEnabled, function(on)
        M.medusaCounterEnabled = on
        if on then M.setupMedusa(player.Character) else M.stopMedusaCounter() end
    end)
    M.setMedusaVisual = setMedusa

    local _, setAutoSwing = uiToggleRow(PMech, "Auto Swing", M.autoSwingEnabled, function(on)
        M.autoSwingEnabled = on
    end)
    M.setAutoSwingVisual = setAutoSwing

    -- PROTECTION (Anti Die expandable: Void / Fling / Drop + No Player Collision)
    uiSectionHeader(PMech, "PROTECTION")
    do
        local dieOn = M.antiDieEnabled == true
        -- empty options => settings-only expand (no blank chip row)
        local _, setAntiDie, _setMode, registerDieSettings = uiExpandToggleRow(
            PMech,
            "Anti Die",
            dieOn,
            {},
            1,
            function(on)
                M.antiDieEnabled = on and true or false
                if on then
                    if M.startAntiDie then M.startAntiDie() end
                else
                    if M.stopAntiDie then M.stopAntiDie() end
                end
                saveCherryConfig()
            end,
            function(_name) end
        )
        M.setAntiDieVisual = setAntiDie

        local settings = Instance.new("Frame")
        settings.Name = "AntiDieSettings"
        settings.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        settings.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        settings.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        local sLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        sLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
        sLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        sLay.Parent = settings

        local _, setAntiVoid = uiToggleRow(settings, "Anti Void", M.antiVoidEnabled, function(on)
            if on then M.startAntiVoid() else M.stopAntiVoid() end
            if M.setAntiVoidVisual then M.setAntiVoidVisual(M.antiVoidEnabled) end
            saveCherryConfig()
        end)
        M.setAntiVoidVisual = setAntiVoid

        local _, setAntiFling = uiToggleRow(settings, "Anti Fling", M.antiFlingEnabled, function(on)
            if on then M.startAntiFling() else M.stopAntiFling() end
            if M.setAntiFlingVisual then M.setAntiFlingVisual(M.antiFlingEnabled) end
            saveCherryConfig()
        end)
        M.setAntiFlingVisual = setAntiFling

        local _, setAntiDrop = uiToggleRow(settings, "Anti Drop", M.antiDropEnabled, function(on)
            if on then M.startAntiDrop() else M.stopAntiDrop() end
            if M.setAntiDropVisual then M.setAntiDropVisual(M.antiDropEnabled) end
            saveCherryConfig()

            if on then M.startNoPlayerCollision() else M.stopNoPlayerCollision() end
            if M.setNoPlayerCollisionVisual then M.setNoPlayerCollisionVisual(M.noPlayerCollisionEnabled) end
            saveCherryConfig()
        end)
        M.setNoPlayerCollisionVisual = setNoCol

        if type(registerDieSettings) == "function" then
            registerDieSettings("_settings", settings)

            settings.Parent = PMech
        end
    end

    -- AIMBOT (Adapt: Normal / Anti Bypass + speeds)
    uiSectionHeader(PMech, "AIMBOT")
    do

        local _, setModeUI = uiChoiceRow(PMech, "Aimbot Mode", {"Normal", "Anti Bypass"}, modeIdx, function(name)
            M.aimbotMode = name
            if M.autoBatEnabled then
                M.stopBatAimbot()
                M.startBatAimbot()
            end
            if M._refreshAimbotSpeedUI then M._refreshAimbotSpeedUI() end
            saveCherryConfig()
        end)
        M.setAimbotModeUI = setModeUI
    end
    do
        local function curNormal()
            if M.aimbotMode == "Anti Bypass" then return tonumber(M.antiBypassAimbotSpeed) or 58 end
            return tonumber(M.AIMBOT_SPEED) or 58
        end
        local function curLagger()
            if M.aimbotMode == "Anti Bypass" then return tonumber(M.antiBypassLaggerAimbotSpeed) or 40 end
            return tonumber(M.LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED) or 40
        end
        local _, spdBox = uiNumberRow(PMech, "Aimbot Speed", curNormal(), 1, 200, function(v)
            if M.aimbotMode == "Anti Bypass" then
                M.antiBypassAimbotSpeed = v
            else
                M.AIMBOT_SPEED = v
            end
            saveCherryConfig()
        end)
        local _, lagBox = uiNumberRow(PMech, "Lagger Aimbot Speed", curLagger(), 1, 200, function(v)
            if M.aimbotMode == "Anti Bypass" then
                M.antiBypassLaggerAimbotSpeed = v
            else
                M.LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED = v
            end
            saveCherryConfig()
        end)
        M._refreshAimbotSpeedUI = function()
            if spdBox then spdBox.Text = tostring(curNormal()) end
            if lagBox then lagBox.Text = tostring(curLagger()) end
        end
    end

                    M.startBypassAimbot()
                else
                    M.stopBypassAimbot()
                end
                if M.mobBtnRefs and M.mobBtnRefs.bypass then M.mobBtnRefs.bypass(M.bypassAimbotEnabled) end
                saveCherryConfig()
            end
        )
        M.setBypassVisual = function(state)
            if setTPBatOn then pcall(function() setTPBatOn(state and true or false) end) end
        end
